from app.postgres_integration import (
    IntegrationPathError,
    IntegrationPaths,
    SnapshotManifest,
)


def test_integration_paths_are_confined_to_project_directory() -> None:
    paths = IntegrationPaths.from_root(
        Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD/3d66-label-system-integration")
    )

    paths.assert_safe()

    assert paths.root == Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD/3d66-label-system-integration")
    assert paths.pgdata == paths.root / "pgdata"
    assert paths.socket_dir == paths.root / "socket"
    assert paths.logs == paths.root / "logs"
    assert paths.snapshots == paths.root / "snapshots"
    assert paths.checkpoints == paths.root / "checkpoints"
    assert paths.reports == paths.root / "reports"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "unsafe",
    [
        Path("/"),
        Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD"),
        Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD/unrelated-project"),
        Path("/tmp/3d66-label-system-integration"),
    ],
)
def test_integration_paths_reject_broad_or_wrong_roots(unsafe: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(IntegrationPathError, match="项目专属目录"):
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(unsafe).assert_safe()


def test_integration_paths_reject_symlinked_child(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    outside = tmp_path / "outside"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    outside.mkdir()
    (root / "logs").symlink_to(outside, target_is_directory=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)

    with pytest.raises(IntegrationPathError, match="符号链接"):
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(root).assert_safe()


def test_disk_report_counts_project_bytes_and_free_space(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "small.bin").write_bytes(b"1234567")
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )

    report = postgres_integration.integration_disk_report(
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    )

    assert report == {
        "root": str(root.resolve()),
        "project_used_bytes": 7,
        "project_limit_bytes": 120 * 1024**3,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": 500 * 1024**3,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": 300 * 1024**3,
        "minimum_free_bytes": 40 * 1024**3,
    }


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("project_bytes", "free_bytes", "message"),
    [
        (120 * 1024**3, 300 * 1024**3, "120 GiB"),
        (1, 40 * 1024**3 - 1, "40 GiB"),
    ],
)
def test_disk_report_fails_closed_at_capacity_limits(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    project_bytes: int,
    free_bytes: int,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration,
        "_directory_size",
        lambda _root: project_bytes,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=500 * 1024**3 - free_bytes,
            free=free_bytes,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(postgres_integration.IntegrationCapacityError, match=message):
        postgres_integration.integration_disk_report(IntegrationPaths.from_root(root))


def test_initialize_cluster_writes_socket_only_private_configuration(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    volume.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    def fake_run(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
        commands.append(tuple(command))
        if command[0].endswith("initdb"):
            pgdata = Path(command[command.index("--pgdata") + 1])
            pgdata.mkdir()
            (pgdata / "PG_VERSION").write_text("16\n", encoding="utf-8")
            (pgdata / "postgresql.conf").write_text(
                "# generated by initdb\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
        return CompletedProcess(command, 0, "", "")

    paths = IntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    binaries = postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries(
        initdb=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16/bin/initdb"),
        pg_ctl=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16/bin/pg_ctl"),
        postgres=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16/bin/postgres"),
        psql=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16/bin/psql"),
        createdb=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16/bin/createdb"),
    )

    postgres_integration.initialize_postgres(
        paths,
        binaries,
        run_command=fake_run,
    )

    assert len(commands) == 1
    assert commands[0][0] == str(binaries.initdb)
    assert "--auth-local=trust" in commands[0]
    assert "--auth-host=reject" in commands[0]
    assert paths.root.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o700
    assert paths.socket_dir.stat().st_mode & 0o777 == 0o700
    rendered = (paths.pgdata / "postgresql.auto.conf").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "listen_addresses = ''" in rendered
    assert f"unix_socket_directories = '{paths.socket_dir}'" in rendered
    assert "unix_socket_permissions = 0700" in rendered
    assert "ssl = off" in rendered


def test_initialize_cluster_is_idempotent_for_postgres_16(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    pgdata = root / "pgdata"
    pgdata.mkdir(parents=True)
    (pgdata / "PG_VERSION").write_text("16\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    postgres_integration.initialize_postgres(
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
        postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(tmp_path / "pg16"),
        run_command=lambda command: commands.append(tuple(command)),
    )

    assert commands == []


def test_initialize_cluster_rejects_existing_wrong_major_version(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    pgdata = root / "pgdata"
    pgdata.mkdir(parents=True)
    (pgdata / "PG_VERSION").write_text("15\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(postgres_integration.PostgresIntegrationError, match="16"):
        postgres_integration.initialize_postgres(
            IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
            postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(tmp_path / "pg16"),
            run_command=lambda _command: pytest.fail("不得重建已有集群"),
        )


def test_preflight_requires_apfs_and_postgres_16(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    volume.mkdir()
    root.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )

    filesystem_probes: list[Path] = []

    def filesystem_type(path: Path) -> str:
        filesystem_probes.append(path)
        return "apfs"

    report = postgres_integration.preflight_postgres(
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
        postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(tmp_path / "pg16"),
        filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
        run_command=lambda command: CompletedProcess(
            command,
            0,
            "postgres (PostgreSQL) 16.10\n",
            "",
        ),
        require_executables=False,
    )

    assert report["filesystem"] == "apfs"
    assert report["postgres_major"] == 16
    assert report["socket_only"] is True
    assert report["project_used_bytes"] == 0
    assert filesystem_probes == [volume.resolve()]


def test_preflight_rejects_non_apfs_volume(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    volume.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(postgres_integration.PostgresIntegrationError, match="APFS"):
        postgres_integration.preflight_postgres(
            IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
            postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(tmp_path / "pg16"),
            filesystem_type=lambda _path: "exfat",
            run_command=lambda _command: pytest.fail("文件系统不合格时不应探测版本"),
            require_executables=False,
        )


def test_start_and_stop_use_only_the_project_cluster(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    pgdata = root / "pgdata"
    pgdata.mkdir(parents=True)
    (pgdata / "PG_VERSION").write_text("16\n", encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []

    def fake_run(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
        commands.append(tuple(command))
        return CompletedProcess(command, 0, "server is running\n", "")

    paths = IntegrationPaths.from_root(root)
    binaries = postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(tmp_path / "pg16")
    postgres_integration.start_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=fake_run)
    postgres_integration.stop_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=fake_run)

    assert commands == [
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "--log",
            str(paths.logs / "postgres-startup.log"),
            "--wait",
            "start",
        ),
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "--mode=fast",
            "--wait",
            "stop",
        ),
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("returncode", "running"), [(0, True), (3, False)])
def test_cluster_status_maps_pg_ctl_exit_codes(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
    returncode: int,
    running: bool,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    (root / "pgdata").mkdir(parents=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)

    status = postgres_integration.postgres_status(
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
        postgres_integration.PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(tmp_path / "pg16"),
        run_command=lambda command: CompletedProcess(
            command,
            returncode,
            "server is running" if running else "no server running",
            "",
        ),
    )

    assert status["running"] is running
    assert status["pgdata"] == str(root / "pgdata")


def test_cli_preflight_outputs_machine_readable_report(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    prefix = tmp_path / "pg16"
    binary_dir = prefix / "bin"
    volume.mkdir()
    binary_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    for name in ("initdb", "pg_ctl", "postgres", "psql", "createdb"):
        binary = binary_dir / name
        binary.write_text("test executable\n", encoding="utf-8")
        binary.chmod(0o700)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = postgres_integration.cli_main(
        [
            "preflight",
            "--root",
            str(root),
            "--postgres-prefix",
            str(prefix),
        ],
        filesystem_type=lambda _path: "apfs",
        run_command=lambda command: CompletedProcess(
            command,
            0,
            "postgres (PostgreSQL) 16.10\n",
            "",
        ),
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    payload = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert payload["command"] == "preflight"
    assert payload["status"] == "ok"
    assert payload["root"] == str(root.resolve())
    assert payload["filesystem"] == "apfs"
    assert payload["postgres_major"] == 16


def test_macos_filesystem_probe_reads_diskutil_plist(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
    plist = """<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
    <!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
    <plist version="1.0"><dict>
      <key>FilesystemName</key><string>APFS</string>
      <key>FilesystemType</key><string>apfs</string>
    </dict></plist>
    """

    def fake_run(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
        commands.append(tuple(command))
        return CompletedProcess(command, 0, plist, "")

    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "_default_run_command", fake_run)

    assert (
        postgres_integration._default_filesystem_type(Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD"))
        == "apfs"
    )
    assert commands == [
        ("/usr/sbin/diskutil", "info", "-plist", "/Volumes/WorkSSD")
    ]


def test_snapshot_copy_only_promotes_a_verified_partial(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    final = tmp_path / "source.db"
    partial = tmp_path / "source.db.partial"
    partial.write_bytes(b"source")

    manifest = postgres_integration.promote_verified_snapshot(
        partial,
        final,
        expected_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"source").hexdigest(),
        expected_size=6,
        source="test-host:/data/source.db",
    )

    assert manifest == SnapshotManifest(
        path=final,
        size_bytes=6,
        sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"source").hexdigest(),
        source="test-host:/data/source.db",
    )
    assert final.read_bytes() == b"source"
    assert stat.S_IMODE(final.stat().st_mode) == 0o400
    assert not partial.exists()
    postgres_integration.verify_snapshot(manifest)


def test_snapshot_hash_mismatch_never_replaces_existing_final(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    final = tmp_path / "source.db"
    partial = tmp_path / "source.db.partial"
    final.write_bytes(b"approved")
    partial.write_bytes(b"corrupt")

    with pytest.raises(
        postgres_integration.SnapshotVerificationError,
        match="SHA-256",
    ):
        postgres_integration.promote_verified_snapshot(
            partial,
            final,
            expected_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"expected").hexdigest(),
            expected_size=8,
            source="test-host:/data/source.db",
        )

    assert final.read_bytes() == b"approved"
    assert partial.read_bytes() == b"corrupt"


def test_verify_snapshot_detects_changes_after_promotion(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    final = tmp_path / "source.db"
    partial = tmp_path / "source.db.partial"
    partial.write_bytes(b"source")
    manifest = postgres_integration.promote_verified_snapshot(
        partial,
        final,
        expected_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"source").hexdigest(),
        expected_size=6,
        source="test-host:/data/source.db",
    )
    final.chmod(0o600)
    final.write_bytes(b"changed")

    with pytest.raises(
        postgres_integration.SnapshotVerificationError,
        match="大小|SHA-256|只读",
    ):
        postgres_integration.verify_snapshot(manifest)


class _FakeSshProcess:
    def __init__(self, payload: bytes, *, returncode: int = 0) -> None:
        self.stdout = BytesIO(payload)
        self.stderr = BytesIO(b"ssh failed" if returncode else b"")
        self._returncode = returncode

    def wait(self) -> int:
        return self._returncode


def test_snapshot_ssh_copy_streams_to_partial_then_promotes(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
    payload = b"verified snapshot"

    def fake_popen(command: Sequence[str], **_kwargs: object) -> _FakeSshProcess:
        commands.append(tuple(command))
        return _FakeSshProcess(payload)

    manifest = postgres_integration.copy_snapshot_from_ssh(
        IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
        remote_host="readonly@test-host",
        remote_path=Path("/data/source.db"),
        expected_sha256=hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest(),
        expected_size=len(payload),
        sudo_read=True,
        popen_factory=fake_popen,
    )

    assert manifest.path == root / "snapshots" / "source.db"
    assert manifest.path.read_bytes() == payload
    assert commands == [
        (
            "/usr/bin/ssh",
            "-o",
            "BatchMode=yes",
            "--",
            "readonly@test-host",
            "sudo -n cat -- /data/source.db",
        )
    ]


def test_cli_copy_snapshot_outputs_verified_manifest(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )
    payload = b"cli snapshot"
    output = StringIO()

    exit_code = postgres_integration.cli_main(
        [
            "copy-snapshot",
            "--root",
            str(root),
            "--remote-host",
            "readonly@test-host",
            "--remote-path",
            "/data/source.db",
            "--expected-sha256",
            hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest(),
            "--expected-size",
            str(len(payload)),
        ],
        popen_factory=lambda _command, **_kwargs: _FakeSshProcess(payload),
        output=output,
    )

    assert exit_code == 0
    rendered = json.loads(output.getvalue())
    assert rendered["command"] == "copy-snapshot"
    assert rendered["status"] == "ok"
    assert rendered["size_bytes"] == len(payload)
    assert rendered["sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest()
    assert rendered["path"] == str(root / "snapshots" / "source.db")


def test_snapshot_copy_rejects_a_symlinked_ssh_identity(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    volume = tmp_path / "WorkSSD"
    root = volume / "3d66-label-system-integration"
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    identity = tmp_path / "identity"
    identity.write_text("fixture", encoding="utf-8")
    identity.chmod(0o600)
    linked_identity = tmp_path / "identity-link"
    linked_identity.symlink_to(identity)
    monkeypatch.setattr(postgres_integration, "INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT", volume)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        postgres_integration.shutil,
        "disk_usage",
        lambda _path: SimpleNamespace(
            total=500 * 1024**3,
            used=200 * 1024**3,
            free=300 * 1024**3,
        ),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        postgres_integration.SnapshotVerificationError,
        match="身份文件",
    ):
        postgres_integration.copy_snapshot_from_ssh(
            IntegrationPaths.from_root(root),
            remote_host="readonly@test-host",
            remote_path=Path("/data/source.db"),
            expected_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"source").hexdigest(),
            expected_size=6,
            identity_file=linked_identity,
            popen_factory=lambda *_args, **_kwargs: pytest.fail(
                "身份文件不安全时不得启动 SSH"
            ),
        )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_postgres_invariants.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import tempfile
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine
from sqlalchemy.exc import DBAPIError

from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.invariant_catalog import INVARIANT_CATALOG
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline
from app.migrations.postgres_invariants import INVARIANT_OWNERS
from app.migrations.postgres_invariants import install_postgres_invariants


def _final_sqlite_trigger_names() -> set[str]:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(
        prefix="labelsystem-trigger-inventory-"
    ) as temporary:
        database = Path(temporary) / "inventory.db"
        engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database}")
        try:
            Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
            with engine.begin() as connection:
                run_migrations(connection)
            with engine.connect() as connection:
                return {
                    str(row[0])
                    for row in connection.execute(
                        text(
                            "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master "
                            "WHERE type='trigger'"
                        )
                    )
                }
        finally:
            engine.dispose()


def test_every_final_sqlite_trigger_has_an_explicit_postgres_owner() -> None:
    sqlite_triggers = _final_sqlite_trigger_names()

    assert len(sqlite_triggers) == 117
    assert set(INVARIANT_OWNERS) == sqlite_triggers
    assert {owner.kind for owner in INVARIANT_OWNERS.values()} <= {
        "postgres_trigger",
        "orm_guard",
        "migration_only",
    }
    assert all(owner.rationale.strip() for owner in INVARIANT_OWNERS.values())


def test_postgres_owner_projection_remains_unchanged() -> None:
    assert Counter(
        contract.owner for contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.values()
    ) == {
        "database_trigger": 48,
        "orm_guard": 69,
    }
    assert {
        name
        for name, owner in INVARIANT_OWNERS.items()
        if owner.kind == "postgres_trigger"
    } == {
        name
        for name, contract in INVARIANT_CATALOG.items()
        if contract.owner == "database_trigger"
    }


def test_postgres_renderer_consumes_shared_trigger_semantics_unchanged() -> None:
    statements: list[str] = []
    connection = SimpleNamespace(
        dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="postgresql"),
        exec_driver_sql=statements.append,
    )

    install_postgres_invariants(connection)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    create_statements = [
        statement
        for statement in statements
        if statement.startswith('CREATE TRIGGER "')
    ]
    assert len(create_statements) == 48
    assert any(
        '"trg_audit_events_no_update" BEFORE UPDATE '
        'ON "audit_events"' in statement
        and "labelsystem_reject_change('audit_events is immutable')" in statement
        for statement in create_statements
    )
    assert any(
        '"trg_baseline_truth_revisions_no_delete" BEFORE DELETE '
        'ON "baseline_truth_revisions"' in statement
        and "labelsystem_reject_change('truth revision cannot be deleted')"
        in statement
        for statement in create_statements
    )


def test_postgres_model_invocation_identity_guard_allows_settlement_updates() -> None:
    statements: list[str] = []
    connection = SimpleNamespace(
        dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="postgresql"),
        exec_driver_sql=statements.append,
    )

    install_postgres_invariants(connection)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    function_statement = next(
        statement
        for statement in statements
        if "labelsystem_freeze_workflow_model_invocation_identity" in statement
        and "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION" in statement
    )
    trigger_statement = next(
        statement
        for statement in statements
        if statement.startswith(
            'CREATE TRIGGER "trg_workflow_model_invocations_identity_frozen"'
        )
    )
    for frozen_column in (
        "baseline_run_id",
        "production_run_id",
        "step_attempt_id",
        "invocation_key",
        "node_instance_id",
        "provider",
        "model_id",
        "reserved_input_tokens",
        "reserved_output_tokens",
        "pricing_snapshot_json",
        "created_at",
    ):
        assert f"OLD.{frozen_column} IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.{frozen_column}" in (
            function_statement
        )
    for settlement_column in (
        "pricing_tier_key",
        "actual_cost_micros",
        "input_tokens",
        "output_tokens",
        "status",
    ):
        assert f"OLD.{settlement_column}" not in function_statement
    assert (
        "EXECUTE FUNCTION "
        "labelsystem_freeze_workflow_model_invocation_identity()"
    ) in trigger_statement


def test_postgres_baseline_installs_append_only_audit_guards(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        connection.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO audit_events "
                "(event_key, category, action, subject_type, subject_id, actor, "
                "payload_json, created_at) VALUES "
                "('pg-invariant-audit', 'runtime', 'created', 'run', '1', "
                "'original', '{}', now())"
            )
        )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="immutable"):
        with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "UPDATE audit_events SET actor='changed' "
                    "WHERE event_key='pg-invariant-audit'"
                )
            )

    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="cannot be deleted"):
        with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "DELETE FROM audit_events "
                    "WHERE event_key='pg-invariant-audit'"
                )
            )


def test_postgres_baseline_installs_every_database_owned_invariant(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        installed = {
            str(row[0])
            for row in connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT tgname FROM pg_trigger "
                    "WHERE NOT tgisinternal AND tgname LIKE 'trg_%'"
                )
            )
        }

    expected = {
        name
        for name, owner in INVARIANT_OWNERS.items()
        if owner.kind == "postgres_trigger"
    }
    assert len(expected) == 47
    assert installed == expected

===== FILE backend/tests/test_postgres_reconciliation.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Column, DateTime, Float, Text, create_engine, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline
from app.postgres_importer import import_sqlite_snapshot
from app.postgres_reconciliation import (
    ReconciliationError,
    assert_critical_invariants,
    canonical_database_value,
    reconcile_databases,
    schema_contract_compatible,
)


def _workflow_source(path: Path) -> Path:
    source = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    now = datetime(2026, 8, 28, 6, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(source)
        with source.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                Base.metadata.tables["workflow_definitions"].insert(),
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "workflow_key": "reconcile-fixture",
                    "name": "对账夹具",
                    "description": "",
                    "owner": "test",
                    "allowed_categories_json": '["knowledge_graph"]',
                    "status": "active",
                    "created_by": "test",
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            connection.execute(
                Base.metadata.tables["workflow_versions"].insert(),
                {
                    "id": 1,
                    "workflow_definition_id": 1,
                    "version": "native-v1",
                    "status": "active",
                    "workflow_schema_version": "workflow-v3",
                    "step_manifest_json": '[{"b":2,"a":1}]',
                    "edge_manifest_json": "[]",
                    "input_schema_json": "{}",
                    "output_schema_json": "{}",
                    "resource_policy_json": "{}",
                    "canonical_hash": "0" * 64,
                    "validation_report_json": "{}",
                    "created_by": "test",
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
    finally:
        source.dispose()
    path.chmod(0o400)
    return path


def test_json_key_order_does_not_change_reconciliation_bytes() -> None:
    column = Column("payload_json", Text)

    assert canonical_database_value('{"b":2,"a":1}', column) == (
        canonical_database_value('{"a":1,"b":2}', column)
    )


def test_cross_dialect_scalar_values_have_one_canonical_form() -> None:
    assert canonical_database_value(1, Column("enabled", Boolean)) == (
        canonical_database_value(True, Column("enabled", Boolean))
    )
    assert canonical_database_value(
        "2026-08-28 06:00:00.000000",
        Column("created_at", DateTime(timezone=True)),
    ) == canonical_database_value(
        datetime(2026, 8, 28, 6, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        Column("created_at", DateTime(timezone=True)),
    )
    assert canonical_database_value(1.0, Column("score", Float)) == (
        canonical_database_value(1, Column("score", Float))
    )


def test_schema_contract_ignores_physical_column_order_but_not_membership() -> None:
    assert schema_contract_compatible(
        source_columns=["id", "legacy", "new_field"],
        target_columns=["id", "new_field", "legacy"],
        source_primary_key=["id"],
        target_primary_key=["id"],
    )
    assert not schema_contract_compatible(
        source_columns=["id", "legacy"],
        target_columns=["id", "new_field", "legacy"],
        source_primary_key=["id"],
        target_primary_key=["id"],
    )


def test_critical_workflow_row_mismatch_blocks_acceptance(
    pg_engine: Engine,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = _workflow_source(tmp_path / "source.db")
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
    import_sqlite_snapshot(
        source,
        pg_engine,
        tmp_path / "checkpoints",
        batch_size=10,
    )
    initial = reconcile_databases(source, pg_engine)
    assert_critical_invariants(initial)

    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM workflow_versions WHERE id=1"))

    report = reconcile_databases(source, pg_engine)
    with pytest.raises(ReconciliationError, match="workflow_versions"):
        assert_critical_invariants(report)

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import inspect, text
from sqlalchemy import CheckConstraint, UniqueConstraint
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateIndex, CreateTable

from app import database as database_module
from app import models  # noqa: F401
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import (
    PostgresBaselineError,
    assert_schema_version,
    create_postgres_baseline,
    postgres_metadata,
    upgrade_postgres_runtime_foundation,
)
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION = max(migration.version for migration in MIGRATIONS)
MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS = {
    "fidelity_mode",
    "fidelity_value",

def test_postgres_metadata_carries_foreach_and_keyed_invocation_identity() -> None:
    metadata = postgres_metadata()
    foreach = metadata.tables["workflow_foreach_items"]
    invocations = metadata.tables["workflow_model_invocations"]

    assert {
        "production_run_id",
        "parent_step_attempt_id",
        "item_key",
        "input_hash",
        "output_hash",
    } <= set(foreach.c.keys())
    assert "invocation_key" in invocations.c
    unique_columns = {
        tuple(column.name for column in constraint.columns)
        for constraint in invocations.constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, UniqueConstraint)
    }
    assert ("step_attempt_id", "invocation_key") in unique_columns
    assert ("step_attempt_id",) not in unique_columns


def test_postgres_upgrade_adds_foreach_and_keyed_invocation_identity(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE workflow_foreach_items")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE workflow_model_invocations DROP CONSTRAINT "
            "uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE workflow_model_invocations DROP COLUMN invocation_key"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "ALTER TABLE workflow_model_invocations ADD CONSTRAINT "
            "uq_workflow_model_invocations_step_attempt "
            "UNIQUE (step_attempt_id)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 95"
        )

        upgrade_postgres_runtime_foundation(connection)

        inspector = inspect(connection)
        assert "workflow_foreach_items" in inspector.get_table_names()
        assert "invocation_key" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("workflow_model_invocations")
        }
        unique_constraints = {
            item["name"]: tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspector.get_unique_constraints(
                "workflow_model_invocations"
            )
        }
        assert unique_constraints[
            "uq_workflow_model_invocations_attempt_key"
        ] == ("step_attempt_id", "invocation_key")
        assert "uq_workflow_model_invocations_step_attempt" not in unique_constraints


def test_postgres_metadata_carries_vision_profile_and_media_evidence() -> None:
    metadata = postgres_metadata()
    assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in metadata.tables["model_configs"].c
    assert "vision_capability_profile_json" in metadata.tables[
        "model_registry_entries"
    ].c
    assert "pricing_contract_json" in metadata.tables["model_configs"].c
    assert "pricing_contract_json" in metadata.tables[
        "model_registry_entries"
    ].c
    assert {
        "pricing_snapshot_json",
        "pricing_tier_key",
        "actual_cost_micros",
    } <= set(metadata.tables["workflow_model_invocations"].c.keys())
    assert MEDIA_EVIDENCE_COLUMNS <= {
        column.name
        for column in metadata.tables["workflow_model_media_usage"].c
    }
    constraint_names = {
        constraint.name
        for table_name in (
            "model_configs",
            "model_registry_entries",
            "workflow_model_invocations",
            "workflow_model_media_usage",
        )
        for constraint in metadata.tables[table_name].constraints
        if isinstance(constraint, CheckConstraint)
    }
    assert {
        "ck_model_configs_vision_capability_profile",
        "ck_model_registry_vision_capability_profile",
        "ck_model_configs_pricing_contract",
        "ck_model_registry_pricing_contract",
        "ck_workflow_model_invocations_pricing_snapshot",
        "ck_workflow_model_invocations_actual_cost",
        "ck_workflow_model_media_vision_policy",
        "ck_workflow_model_media_vision_hashes",
        "ck_workflow_model_media_transport_metrics",
    } <= constraint_names


def test_postgres_metadata_translates_integer_boolean_defaults() -> None:
    metadata = postgres_metadata()

    ddl = "\n".join(
        str(CreateTable(table).compile(dialect=postgresql.dialect()))
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
    ).lower()

    assert "boolean default 0" not in ddl
    assert "boolean default 1" not in ddl
    assert "budget_settled boolean default false" in ddl
    assert "is_admin boolean default false" in ddl


def test_postgres_metadata_preserves_every_sqlite_partial_index_predicate() -> None:
    metadata = postgres_metadata()
    partial_indexes = [
        index
        for table in metadata.tables.values()
        for index in table.indexes
        if index.dialect_options["sqlite"].get("where") is not None
    ]

    assert len(partial_indexes) == 9
    for index in partial_indexes:
        ddl = str(
            CreateIndex(index).compile(dialect=postgresql.dialect())
        ).lower()
        assert " where " in ddl, index.name


def test_version_identifier_columns_accept_existing_long_operator_versions() -> None:
    version_columns = {
        ("prompt_versions", "version"),
        ("prompt_versions", "rubric_version"),
        ("evaluation_category_profiles", "rubric_version"),
        ("strategy_bundles", "prompt_a_version"),
        ("strategy_bundles", "prompt_b_version"),
        ("evaluation_results", "prompt_a_version"),
        ("evaluation_results", "prompt_b_version"),
        ("optimization_case_queue", "prompt_version"),
    }

    for table_name, column_name in version_columns:
        length = Base.metadata.tables[table_name].c[column_name].type.length
        assert length is not None and length >= 120, (table_name, column_name)


def test_postgres_baseline_creates_every_mapped_table_idempotently(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)

    actual = set(inspect(pg_engine).get_table_names())

    assert set(Base.metadata.tables) <= actual
    assert "schema_migrations" in actual
    with pg_engine.connect() as connection:
        assert (
            connection.scalar(text("SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"))
            == LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION
        )


def test_postgres_baseline_rejects_non_object_runtime_audit_details(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "INSERT INTO runtime_audit_events "
                    "(event_key, entity_type, entity_key, action, actor, "
                    "details_json, created_at) VALUES "
                    "(:event_key, 'run', 'run-1', 'created', 'test', '[]', now())"
                ),
                {"event_key": "invalid-json-shape"},
            )

    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO runtime_audit_events "
                "(event_key, entity_type, entity_key, action, actor, "
                "details_json, created_at) VALUES "
                "('valid-json-shape', 'run', 'run-1', 'created', 'test', '{}', now())"
            )
        )


def test_assert_schema_version_requires_the_exact_recorded_baseline(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        assert_schema_version(connection, expected=LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION)

        with pytest.raises(
            PostgresBaselineError,
            match=(
                rf"期望 {LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION + 1}，"
                rf"实际 {LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION}"
            ),
        ):
            assert_schema_version(
                connection,
                expected=LATEST_SCHEMA_VERSION + 1,
            )

def test_postgres_baseline_loads_models_in_a_clean_process(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    environment = os.environ.copy()
    environment["CLEAN_POSTGRES_URL"] = pg_engine.url.render_as_string(
        hide_password=False
    )
    backend_root = str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1])
    environment["PYTHONPATH"] = backend_root

    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            "-c",
            "from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect; "
            "from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline; "
            "import os; "
            "engine=create_engine(os.environ['CLEAN_POSTGRES_URL']); "
            "connection=engine.connect(); transaction=connection.begin(); "
            "create_postgres_baseline(connection); transaction.commit(); "
            "print(len(inspect(engine).get_table_names())); "
            "connection.close(); engine.dispose()",
        ],
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        env=environment,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 0, completed.stderr
    assert completed.stdout.strip() == str(len(Base.metadata.tables) + 1)


def test_postgres_runtime_foundation_contracts_are_enforced(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
        inspector = inspect(connection)
        assert {"workflow_snapshot_blobs", "runtime_outbox_events"} <= set(
            inspector.get_table_names()
        )
        assert "snapshot_blob_id" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("production_runs")
        }
        assert "dead_lettered_at" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("runtime_dispatch_items")
        }
        connection.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_snapshot_blobs "
                "(snapshot_hash, canonical_size_bytes, compressed_size_bytes, "
                "compression, payload_bytes, created_at) VALUES "
                "(:snapshot_hash, 2, 22, 'gzip', :payload, now())"
            ),
            {"snapshot_hash": "a" * 64, "payload": b"payload"},
        )

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_snapshot_blobs "
                    "(snapshot_hash, canonical_size_bytes, compressed_size_bytes, "
                    "compression, payload_bytes, created_at) VALUES "
                    "(:snapshot_hash, 2, 22, 'gzip', :payload, now())"
                ),
                {"snapshot_hash": "a" * 64, "payload": b"duplicate"},
            )


def test_postgres_baseline_upgrades_existing_v81_runtime_tables(
    pg_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE production_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE production_step_attempts (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO production_step_attempts(id) VALUES (7)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            """
            CREATE TABLE runtime_dispatch_items (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                step_attempt_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE
                    REFERENCES production_step_attempts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                queue_class VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
                priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 50,
                status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
                available_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                CONSTRAINT ck_runtime_dispatch_items_status
                    CHECK(status IN ('queued','leased','completed','canceled'))
            )
            """
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO runtime_dispatch_items"
            "(id, step_attempt_id, queue_class, priority, status) "
            "VALUES (9, 7, 'validation', 80, 'queued')"
        )

        create_postgres_baseline(connection)

        inspector = inspect(connection)
        assert "snapshot_blob_id" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("production_runs")
        }
        assert "dead_lettered_at" in {
            column["name"]
            for column in inspector.get_columns("runtime_dispatch_items")
        }
        preserved = connection.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT id, step_attempt_id, priority, status, attempt_count, "
                "max_attempts FROM runtime_dispatch_items"
            )
        ).one()
        assert tuple(preserved) == (9, 7, 80, "queued", 0, 3)
        connection.execute(
            text(
                "UPDATE runtime_dispatch_items SET status='dead_lettered', "
                "dead_lettered_at=now() WHERE id=9"
            )
        )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prepare_new_server.py =====
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def module():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('prepare_server', ROOT / 'scripts/prepare-new-server.py')
    loaded = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(loaded)
    return loaded


@pytest.mark.parametrize('stage', [96, 192, 384, 768])
def test_plans_never_enable_production_even_with_sufficient_hardware(stage):
    result = module().prepare(stage, dict(system='Linux', physical_cores=8,
        logical_cpus=16, memory_bytes=64*1024**3))
    assert result['hardware_minimum_met']
    assert not result['production_ready']
    assert result['candidate']['total_slots'] == stage
    assert result['disabled_environment']['INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED'] == '0'
    assert result['disabled_environment']['WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION'] == '0'
    assert result['disabled_environment']['WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION'] == '0'
    assert result['disabled_environment']['WORKER_RUNTIME_VALIDATED_SLOTS'] == '0'
    assert result['disabled_environment']['WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS'] == '0'
    assert result['probe_argv'][-1] == '--split-admission'
    assert '--extended-envelope' in result['probe_argv']
    # 上限要跟常量走，不写死：线上实测 max_connections=512，旧的 200 是过时假设。
    assert result['candidate']['mysql_max_connections'] == 512
    assert result['candidate']['connection_budget'] <= result['candidate']['mysql_max_connections']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('inventory', [dict(), dict(system='Linux', physical_cores=4,
    logical_cpus=8, memory_bytes=16*1024**3)])
def test_unknown_or_small_hardware_cannot_pass_preflight(inventory):
    result = module().prepare(96, inventory)
    assert not result['hardware_minimum_met'] and not result['production_ready']


def test_prepare_cli_is_a_read_only_plan_not_installation(tmp_path):
    inventory = tmp_path / 'inventory.json'
    inventory.write_text('{}')
    command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'scripts/prepare-new-server.py'),
               '--stage', '192', '--inventory-json', str(inventory)]
    result = subprocess.run(command, cwd=tmp_path, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode == 0, result.stderr
    body = json.loads(result.stdout)
    assert body['actions_executed'] == [] and body['production_ready'] is False
    assert sorted(p.name for p in tmp_path.iterdir()) == ['inventory.json']
    bad = subprocess.run(command + ['--deploy'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert bad.returncode != 0

    from test_new_server_acceptance import evidence
    data = evidence()
    data['inventory_complete'] = False
    payload = tmp_path / 'window.json'

    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'scripts/assess-new-server.py'),
        str(payload)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    assert result.returncode == 1, result.stderr
    assert 'inventory_incomplete' in json.loads(result.stdout)['blockers']

    assert 'must-not-echo' not in json.dumps(result)

    path = ROOT / 'docs/verification/new-server-readiness-20260914/compose.disabled.json'
    services = json.loads(path.read_text())['services']
    for service in services.values():
        assert service['profiles'] == ['new-server-after-fence']
        assert service['environment']['INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED'] == '${INCREMENTAL_SLA_ENABLED:-0}'
        assert service['environment']['WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION'] == '${WORKER_RUNTIME_FAIR_SUBMISSION:-0}'
    worker = services['worker-group']
    assert worker['deploy']['replicas'] == '${NEW_SERVER_WORKER_GROUP_REPLICAS:-0}'
    assert worker['environment']['DATABASE_MAX_OVERFLOW'] == '0'
    assert worker['environment']['WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION'] == '${WORKER_RUNTIME_SPLIT_ADMISSION:-0}'

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prepared_media_memo.py =====
"""2026-09-19：同一进程内重复准备同一张冻结图片不再重读 NAS、重解码。"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from PIL import Image

from app import lossless_image_transport as lossless
from app import media
from app import model_media_usage as usage
from app.prepared_media_memo import BoundedMemo, file_identity, sha256_file_memo
from app.vision_input_policy import CustomPreprocess


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def _write_png(path: Path, size=(64, 48)) -> Path:
    image = Image.new("RGB", size, (200, 30, 30))
    image.putpixel((1, 1), (0, 255, 0))
    image.save(path, format="PNG")
    return path


def _capability():
    from tests.test_lossless_image_transport import _capability as capability

    return capability()


def _touch_later(path: Path) -> None:
    stat = path.stat()
    os.utime(path, ns=(stat.st_atime_ns, stat.st_mtime_ns + 2_000_000))


def test_bounded_memo_drops_oldest_quarter_when_full() -> None:
    memo = BoundedMemo(8)
    for index in range(8):
        memo.put(index, index)
    memo.put("new", 1)
    assert len(memo) == 7
    assert memo.get(0) is None and memo.get(1) is None and memo.get("new") == 1


def test_sha256_memo_follows_file_identity(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "a.bin"
    path.write_bytes(b"one")
    calls: list[Path] = []

    def compute(target: Path) -> str:
        calls.append(target)
        return _sha256(target)

    first = sha256_file_memo(path, compute)
    assert sha256_file_memo(path, compute) == first
    assert len(calls) == 1
    path.write_bytes(b"two!")
    assert sha256_file_memo(path, compute) == _sha256(path)
    assert len(calls) == 2
    assert file_identity(tmp_path / "missing") is None


def test_geometry_memo_skips_decode_and_revalidates_on_change(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    source = _write_png(tmp_path / "source.png", size=(400, 300))
    cache_dir = tmp_path / "geometry"
    preprocess = CustomPreprocess(max_width=200, max_height=200)
    kwargs = dict(mime_type="image/png", content_sha256=_sha256(source),
                  cache_dir=cache_dir, preprocess=preprocess)
    first_path, first_mime = media.prepare_vision_geometry(source, **kwargs)
    assert first_mime == "image/png" and first_path.parent == cache_dir

    real_open = media.Image.open
    opened: list[Path] = []

    def counting_open(path, *args, **inner):
        opened.append(Path(path))
        return real_open(path, *args, **inner)

    monkeypatch.setattr(media.Image, "open", counting_open)
    second = media.prepare_vision_geometry(source, **kwargs)
    assert second == (first_path, first_mime)
    # 只剩动图探测那一次 header 打开，原图与派生 PNG 都没有再完整解码
    assert opened == [source]

    # 派生文件一变，备忘作废，重新完整校验并重建
    first_path.write_bytes(b"corrupt")
    opened.clear()
    rebuilt = media.prepare_vision_geometry(source, **kwargs)
    assert rebuilt == (first_path, first_mime)
    with Image.open(first_path) as image:
        assert image.size == (200, 150)
    assert first_path in opened or source in opened

    # 原图身份一变（mtime），同样重新校验
    _touch_later(source)
    opened.clear()
    assert media.prepare_vision_geometry(source, **kwargs) == (first_path, first_mime)
    assert len(opened) >= 2


def test_geometry_memo_remembers_unchanged_source(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    source = _write_png(tmp_path / "small.png", size=(64, 48))
    kwargs = dict(mime_type="image/png", content_sha256=_sha256(source),
                  cache_dir=tmp_path / "geometry",
                  preprocess=CustomPreprocess(max_width=200, max_height=200))
    assert media.prepare_vision_geometry(source, **kwargs) == (source, "image/png")
    real_open = media.Image.open
    opened: list[Path] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(media.Image, "open", lambda p, *a, **k: (opened.append(Path(p)), real_open(p, *a, **k))[1])
    assert media.prepare_vision_geometry(source, **kwargs) == (source, "image/png")
    assert opened == [source]


def test_lossless_memo_reuses_verified_result(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    source = _write_png(tmp_path / "source.png")
    digest = _sha256(source)
    cache = tmp_path / "cache"
    first = lossless.prepare_lossless_transport(source, digest, "image/png", _capability(), cache)
    real_pixels = lossless.normalized_pixels
    decoded: list[Path] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(lossless, "normalized_pixels",
                        lambda p: (decoded.append(Path(p)), real_pixels(p))[1])
    second = lossless.prepare_lossless_transport(source, digest, "image/png", _capability(), cache)
    assert decoded == []
    assert second.cache_outcome in {"hit", "fallback_source"}
    assert second.prepared_path == first.prepared_path
    assert second.sent_sha256 == first.sent_sha256
    assert second.normalized_sent_pixel_hash == first.normalized_sent_pixel_hash
    assert second.evidence() | {"compression_ms": 0, "cache_outcome": "x", "compressor_name": "x"} == (
        first.evidence() | {"compression_ms": 0, "cache_outcome": "x", "compressor_name": "x"})

    # 声明摘要不同（同一文件被另一个冻结摘要引用）不能命中备忘
    with pytest.raises(lossless.LosslessImageTransportError):
        lossless.prepare_lossless_transport(source, "0" * 64, "image/png", _capability(), cache)

    # 发送文件被改动：备忘作废，重新走完整校验
    if first.prepared_path != source:
        first.prepared_path.write_bytes(b"corrupt-cache")
        rebuilt = lossless.prepare_lossless_transport(source, digest, "image/png", _capability(), cache)
        assert decoded, "changed cache file must be re-verified"
        assert rebuilt.sent_sha256 == first.sent_sha256


def test_model_media_usage_sha_memo_tracks_rewrites(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "x.bin"
    path.write_bytes(b"abc")
    assert usage._sha256_file(path) == _sha256(path)
    path.write_bytes(b"abcd")
    assert usage._sha256_file(path) == _sha256(path)


def test_not_smaller_verdict_is_remembered_on_disk(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    source = _write_png(tmp_path / "flat.png")
    digest = _sha256(source)
    cache = tmp_path / "cache"
    runs: list[Path] = []

    def never_smaller(source_path: Path, candidate_path: Path, _mime: str):
        runs.append(source_path)
        candidate_path.write_bytes(source_path.read_bytes() + b"padding")
        return "fake", "v1"

    monkeypatch.setattr(lossless, "_run_optimizer", never_smaller)
    first = lossless.prepare_lossless_transport(source, digest, "image/png", _capability(), cache)
    assert first.cache_outcome == "fallback_source" and first.fallback_reason_code == "candidate_not_smaller"
    assert len(runs) == 1
    markers = list(cache.rglob("*.not-smaller"))
    assert len(markers) == 1
    # a fresh process (memo cleared) must not run the optimizer again
    lossless._TRANSPORT_MEMO.clear()
    second = lossless.prepare_lossless_transport(source, digest, "image/png", _capability(), cache)
    assert len(runs) == 1
    assert second.prepared_path == source and second.fallback_reason_code == "candidate_not_smaller"
    assert second.sent_sha256 == first.sent_sha256

===== FILE backend/tests/test_production_module_prepare.py =====
"""Preparation parsing is pure; no deployment, environment or credential reads."""
import importlib.util
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('module_prepare', Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'deploy/server/prepare_business_cutover.py')
prepare = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(prepare)


def test_runtime_duplicates_cannot_leave_an_old_override_after_the_new_value():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='Duplicate runtime variable: BUILD'):
        prepare.unique_env_values('BUILD=new\nBUILD=old\n')


def test_runtime_unique_values_keep_equals_inside_values_and_skip_comments():
    assert prepare.unique_env_values(' # BUILD=comment\nBUILD=new\nEMPTY=\nVALUE=x=y\n') == {'BUILD':'new', 'EMPTY':'', 'VALUE':'x=y'}


def test_baseline_is_explicit_and_stale_container_env_or_revision_is_rejected():
    env = ('LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA=' + 'b' * 40 + '\n').encode()
    baseline = {'source_sha': 'b' * 40, 'image': 'sha256:' + 'c' * 64,
                'env_sha256': hashlib.sha256(env).hexdigest()}
    rows = [{'Image': baseline['image'], 'State': {'Running': True}} for _ in range(2)]
    assert prepare.verify_baseline(baseline, rows, env)['LABEL_LAB_BUILD_SHA'] == 'b' * 40
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='container identity'):
        prepare.verify_baseline(baseline, [rows[0], dict(rows[1], Image='sha256:' + 'd' * 64)], env)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='container identity'):
        prepare.verify_baseline(baseline, [rows[0]], env)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='environment changed'):
        prepare.verify_baseline(baseline, rows, env + b'NEW=value\n')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='revision changed'):
        prepare.verify_baseline(dict(baseline, source_sha='a' * 40), rows, env)
    for invalid in ({}, dict(baseline, source_sha='short'), dict(baseline, image='mutable:tag'),
                    dict(baseline, env_sha256=None), dict(baseline, extra='value')):
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            prepare.validate_baseline_spec(invalid)


def test_standalone_nginx_check_never_reuses_live_temp_directories_or_logs(tmp_path):
    site = '''server {
    access_log /var/log/nginx/live.access.log;
    error_log /var/log/nginx/live.error.log warn;
    location / {proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8081;}
}'''
    config = prepare.isolated_nginx_check(tmp_path, site)
    assert '/var/log/nginx' not in config and '/var/lib/nginx' not in config
    assert 'proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8081;' in config
    for name in ('client_body', 'proxy', 'fastcgi', 'uwsgi', 'scgi'):
        path = tmp_path / 'nginx-syntax-sandbox' / name
        assert f'{name}_temp_path {path};' in config and path.is_dir()
    assert f'pid {tmp_path}/nginx-syntax-sandbox/nginx.pid;' in config
    with pytest.raises(FileExistsError):
        prepare.isolated_nginx_check(tmp_path, site)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_production_session_security.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fastapi import HTTPException, Response
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from starlette.requests import Request

from app.config import get_settings
from app.database import Base
from app.models import User
from app.security import DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH, hash_password


def _database() -> tuple[Session, object]:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite+pysqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return Session(engine), engine


def _ready_snapshot() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "service": "3d66-label-system",
        "database": {"status": "ok"},
        "workers": {
            "active_worker_count": 1,
            "expected_worker_count": 1,
            "missing_worker_count": 0,
            "worker_capacity_status": "ready",
            "workers": [],
        },
    }


def _settings_from_environment(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> object:
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATA_DIR", str(tmp_path / "data"))
    monkeypatch.delenv("DATABASE_URL_FILE", raising=False)
    monkeypatch.delenv("DATABASE_URL", raising=False)
    get_settings.cache_clear()
    return get_settings()


def test_security_boolean_settings_are_strict_and_production_aware(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_DEPLOY_ENV", "production")
    monkeypatch.setenv("SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE", "true")
    monkeypatch.setenv("ALLOW_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD", "false")

    settings = _settings_from_environment(monkeypatch, tmp_path)

    assert settings.deploy_environment == "production"
    assert settings.session_cookie_secure is True
    assert settings.allow_default_admin_password is False

    monkeypatch.setenv("SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE", "yes")
    get_settings.cache_clear()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE"):
        get_settings()
    get_settings.cache_clear()


def test_login_cookie_is_http_usable_locally_and_secure_when_configured(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    from app import main as main_module

    db, engine = _database()
    try:
        db.add(
            User(
                username="cookie-user",
                password_hash=hash_password("fixture-password"),
                password_initialized=True,
                display_name="Cookie User",
                role="admin",
                is_admin=True,
            )
        )
        db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "settings",
            replace(main_module.settings, session_cookie_secure=False),
        )
        local_response = Response()
        main_module.login(
            main_module.LoginRequest(
                username="cookie-user",
                password="fixture-password",
            ),
            local_response,
            Request({"type": "http", "method": "POST", "path": "/api/auth/login", "headers": []}),
            db,
        )
        assert "secure" not in local_response.headers["set-cookie"].lower()

        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "settings",
            replace(main_module.settings, session_cookie_secure=True),
        )
        production_response = Response()
        main_module.login(
            main_module.LoginRequest(
                username="cookie-user",
                password="fixture-password",
            ),
            production_response,
            Request({"type": "http", "method": "POST", "path": "/api/auth/login", "headers": []}),
            db,
        )
        assert "secure" in production_response.headers["set-cookie"].lower()

        logout_response = Response()
        main_module.logout(logout_response, Request({"type": "http", "method": "POST", "path": "/api/auth/logout", "headers": []}), None, db)
        assert "secure" in logout_response.headers["set-cookie"].lower()
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("secure_cookie", "password_hash", "expected_code"),
    [
        (False, hash_password("not-the-default"), "session_cookie_insecure"),
        (True, DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH, "default_admin_password"),
    ],
)
def test_production_readiness_rejects_session_security_blockers(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    secure_cookie: bool,
    password_hash: str,
    expected_code: str,
) -> None:
    from app import main as main_module

    db, engine = _database()
    try:
        db.add(
            User(
                username="sol",
                password_hash=password_hash,
                display_name="系统管理员",
                role="admin",
                is_admin=True,
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "settings",
            replace(
                main_module.settings,
                deploy_environment="production",
                session_cookie_secure=secure_cookie,
                allow_default_admin_password=False,
            ),
        )
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            main_module,
            "_runtime_health_snapshot",
            lambda _db: _ready_snapshot(),
        )

        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as error:
            main_module.health_ready(db)

        assert error.value.status_code == 503
        assert error.value.detail["code"] == "production_security_not_ready"
        assert expected_code in {
            item["code"] for item in error.value.detail["blockers"]
        }
        serialized = json.dumps(error.value.detail, ensure_ascii=False)
        assert DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH not in serialized
        assert password_hash not in serialized
    finally:
        db.close()
        engine.dispose()
