        db.close()
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_production_title_normalization.py =====
"""Actual run427 title regression, transport validation, and frozen opt-in boundaries."""
import asyncio
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from PIL import Image

from app.doubao import DoubaoClient, DoubaoStructuredOutputError
from app.production_title_normalization import CONFIG_KEY, PROFILE, frozen_title_profile, normalize_production_title
from app.prompt_output_contract import parse_output_contract
from app.strict_prompt_contracts import _model_3d_su_call_a_schema


def contract():
    return parse_output_contract(dict(mode='json_schema', name='model_3d_su_call_a', strict=True,
                                     schema=_model_3d_su_call_a_schema()))


def payload():
    # Fields observed in run427 / asset18696 / invocation96499; remaining
    # contract fields are explicit fixture facts, not reconstructed model evidence.
    schema = contract().schema
    def value(s):
        if s['type'] == 'object': return {k: value(v) for k,v in s['properties'].items()}
        if s['type'] == 'array': return ['visible'] * s.get('minItems', 0)
        if s['type'] in ('integer','number'): return s.get('minimum',0)
        return s.get('enum',[''])[0]
    result = value(schema)
    result['production_fields'].update(title='Hello Kitty美陈',
        seotitle='粉色Hello Kitty主题卡通美陈拱门装置素材',
        tags=['卡通', '美陈装置', '粉色', '拱门造型', 'Hello Kitty主题'])
    return result


def test_actual_failed_title_retains_brand_and_all_visual_facts():
    raw=payload(); p=deepcopy(raw)
    trace=normalize_production_title(contract(),p,PROFILE)
    expected=deepcopy(raw);expected['production_fields']['title']='美陈'
    assert p==expected and raw['production_fields']['title']=='Hello Kitty美陈'
    assert trace[0]['before']=='Hello Kitty美陈' and trace[0]['after']=='美陈'
    assert trace[0]['preserved_text']==[{'text':'Hello Kitty','field':'production_fields.seotitle'}]
    assert normalize_production_title(contract(),p,PROFILE)==[]


def test_missing_brand_added_without_replacing_existing_tags_or_seo():
    p=payload();p['production_fields']['seotitle']='粉色卡通美陈'
    p['production_fields']['tags']=['卡通','美陈','粉色','拱门']
    trace=normalize_production_title(contract(),p,PROFILE)
    assert p['production_fields']['tags']==['卡通','美陈','粉色','拱门','Hello Kitty']
    assert p['production_fields']['seotitle']=='粉色卡通美陈'
    assert trace[1]['before']==['卡通','美陈','粉色','拱门']
    assert trace[1]['after']==p['production_fields']['tags']


@pytest.mark.parametrize('title',['Hello Kitty','超长的纯中文标题不能直接随意截断','Hello Kitty非常长的中文标题不能随意截断','Hello Kitty美陈2号','Hello Kitty（美陈）','3D模型展示非常长的标题','Nike美陈'])
def test_ambiguous_or_valid_titles_not_rewritten(title):
    p=payload();p['production_fields']['title']=title;before=deepcopy(p)
    assert normalize_production_title(contract(),p,PROFILE)==[]
    assert p==before


def test_profile_is_frozen_and_scoped():
    node={'node_instance_id':'prompt_a','config':{'output_contract':{'name':'model_3d_su_call_a'}}}
    assert frozen_title_profile(node,'model_3d_su') is None
    node['config'][CONFIG_KEY]=PROFILE
    assert frozen_title_profile(node,'model_3d_su')==PROFILE
    for category,name,profile in [('inspiration_image','prompt_a',PROFILE),('model_3d_su','prompt_b',PROFILE),('model_3d_su','prompt_a','future-v2')]:
        changed=deepcopy(node);changed['node_instance_id']=name;changed['config'][CONFIG_KEY]=profile
        with pytest.raises(ValueError): frozen_title_profile(changed,category)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('multi',[False,True])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('enabled',[False,True])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('other_invalid',[False,True])
def test_transport_normalizes_before_strict_validation_preserves_raw_and_usage(tmp_path,multi,enabled,other_invalid):
    client=object.__new__(DoubaoClient);client.api_key='fixture'
    client.config=SimpleNamespace(protocol='openai_chat',provider='doubao',model_id='fixture',
        max_tokens=2000,temperature=.1,structured_output=True,max_retries=0,
        production_title_normalization_profile=PROFILE if enabled else None)
    p=payload()
    if other_invalid:p['classification']['primary_category']='INVALID'
    raw_text=json.dumps(p,ensure_ascii=False)
    raw={'choices':[{'finish_reason':'stop','message':{'content':raw_text}}],
         'usage':{'prompt_tokens':100,'completion_tokens':50,'total_tokens':150}}
    calls=[]
    async def post(body):calls.append(body);return deepcopy(raw)
    client._post=post;client._post_serialized=post
    kwargs=dict(max_attempts=1,output_contract=contract())
    if multi:
        path=tmp_path/'image.png';Image.new('RGB',(32,32)).save(path)
        samples=[('target',path,'image/png')]
        request=client.serialize_chat_json_images_request(client.config,'system',samples,user_prompt='user',output_contract=contract())
        operation=client.chat_json_images('system',samples,user_prompt='user',prepared_request=request,**kwargs)
    else:operation=client.chat_json('system','user',**kwargs)
    if not enabled or other_invalid:
        with pytest.raises(DoubaoStructuredOutputError) as caught:asyncio.run(operation)
        assert caught.value.provider_usage['input_tokens']==100
        if enabled:
            assert caught.value.evidence['output_normalization'][0]['before']=='Hello Kitty美陈'
        else:assert not (getattr(caught.value,'evidence',None) or {}).get('output_normalization')
    else:
        response=asyncio.run(operation)
        assert response.parsed['production_fields']['title']=='美陈'
        assert response.raw_text==raw_text and response.raw_payload==raw
        assert response.output_normalization[0]['before']=='Hello Kitty美陈'
        assert (response.input_tokens,response.output_tokens,response.total_tokens)==(100,50,150)
    assert len(calls)==1


def title_manifest():
    from test_workflow_v3 import _valid_manifest
    m=_valid_manifest();m['category_key']='model_3d_su'
    # A managed three-node workflow is sufficient to exercise the real freeze
    # and publication path, without a provider call or production database.
    m['nodes']=[n for n in m['nodes'] if n['kind']!='rule_module']
    a=m['nodes'][1];a['node_instance_id']='prompt_a'
    a['config'].update(structured_output=True,output_contract=contract().model_dump(by_alias=True),**{CONFIG_KEY:PROFILE})
    m['edges']=[{'from':'upstream','to':'prompt_a'},{'from':'prompt_a','to':'downstream'}]
    return m


def test_title_profile_passes_pure_registry_and_rejects_wrong_scope():
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3
    m=title_manifest()
    assert validate_workflow_v3(m)==[]
    for category,profile in [('space_image',PROFILE),('model_3d_su','unknown-v2')]:
        changed=deepcopy(m);changed['category_key']=category;changed['nodes'][1]['config'][CONFIG_KEY]=profile
        errors=validate_workflow_v3(changed)
        assert any(e.code=='workflow_title_normalization_invalid' for e in errors)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('feedback_profile', [None, 'validation-feedback-v2'])
def test_title_profile_survives_actual_immutable_version_creation(tmp_path, feedback_profile):
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    from test_workflow_working_copies import _engine,_seed_managed_dependencies
    from app.models import EvaluationCategoryProfile,ModelRegistryEntry,ModelConfig
    from app.evaluation_components import create_component_asset,save_component_working_copy,generate_component_version
    from app.workflow_working_copies import create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
    engine=_engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            definition,seed=_seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            model=db.get(ModelRegistryEntry,seed['nodes'][1]['config']['model_registry_id'])
            model.protocol='openai_chat'
            db.get(ModelConfig,model.source_model_config_id).protocol='openai_chat'
            definition.allowed_categories_json='["space_image","model_3d_su"]'
            db.add(EvaluationCategoryProfile(category_key='model_3d_su',display_name='3DSU',status='active',allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',preprocess_config_json='{}',pipeline_config_json='{}',created_by='ops'))
            db.flush()
            asset=create_component_asset(db,category_key='model_3d_su',asset_key='prompt_a',kind='prompt',display_name='A',prompt_stage='A',created_by='ops')
            save_component_working_copy(db,asset_id=asset.id,expected_lock_version=1,content={'stage':'A','system_prompt':'Return observed fields.','user_prompt':'Return JSON.','rubric_version':'fixture-v1'},updated_by='ops')
            prompt=generate_component_version(db,asset_id=asset.id,created_by='ops')
            m=title_manifest();m['nodes'][1]['config'].update(asset_key='prompt_a',prompt_version_id=prompt.id,model_registry_id=seed['nodes'][1]['config']['model_registry_id'])
            if feedback_profile:
                m.setdefault('resource_policy',{}).setdefault('source_provenance',{})['retry_feedback_profile']=feedback_profile
            original=deepcopy(m)
            version=create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(db,definition=definition,version='title-test-v1',manifest=m,created_by='ops')
            frozen=persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
            assert frozen['nodes'][1]['config'][CONFIG_KEY]==PROFILE and m==original
            if feedback_profile:
                assert frozen['resource_policy']['source_provenance']['retry_feedback_profile']==feedback_profile
            second=create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version(db,definition=definition,version='title-test-v2',manifest=frozen,created_by='ops')
            assert persisted_workflow_manifest(second)==frozen
    finally:engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_projection_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Iterator

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import (
    LabelRelease,
    LocalProjectionRow,
    ProjectionContract,
    ProjectionManifest,
    PublishedLabel,
    User,
)
from app.projection_contracts import LocalProjectionAdapter


def _contract_payload(*, contract_key: str = "unified-dimension") -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "contract_key": contract_key,
        "target_role": "unified_dimension",
        "table_name": "unified_dimension_table",
        "environment": "local",
        "primary_key": ["content_key"],
        "field_mappings": {
            "content_key": "content_key",
            "category_key": "category_key",
            "label_version": "$label.version",
            "level": "level",
            "score": "score",
            "classification": "classification",
            "dimensions": "dimensions",
            "production_fields": "production_fields",
            "image_quality": "image_quality",
            "media_form": "media_form",
            "asset_version": "provenance.asset_sha256",
            "mechanism_version": "provenance.strategy_bundle_id",
            "model_version": "provenance.model_id",
        },
        "input_versions": {"label_schema_version": "published-label-v1"},
        "mode": "snapshot",
        "idempotency_key_template": "{table_name}:{content_key}:{label_version}",
        "checkpoint": {"kind": "published_label_id"},
        "reconciliation": {"checks": ["row_count", "missing", "payload_hash", "version"]},
        "rollback": {"strategy": "rebuild_previous_published_version"},
        "owner": "tpeng-label-platform",
        "status": "draft",
    }


@contextmanager
def _projection_context() -> Iterator[dict[str, object]]:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        user = User(
            username="projection-owner",
            password_hash="unused",
            display_name="投影合同管理员",
            is_admin=True,
        )
        published_release = LabelRelease(
            release_key="formal-release",
            content_key="content:formal-1",
            category_key="space_image",
            label_schema_version="published-label-v1",
            label_payload_json="{}",
            payload_hash="a" * 64,
            status="published",
            requested_by=user.username,
            approved_by=user.username,
            approved_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
        candidate_release = LabelRelease(
            release_key="candidate-release",
            content_key="content:candidate-1",
            category_key="space_image",
            label_schema_version="published-label-v1",
            label_payload_json=json.dumps({"candidate_mechanism": "must-not-project"}),
            payload_hash="b" * 64,
            status="pending_review",
            requested_by=user.username,
        )
        db.add_all([user, published_release, candidate_release])
        db.flush()
        payload = {
            "schema_version": "published-label-v1",
            "content_key": "content:formal-1",
            "category_key": "space_image",
            "level": "L2",
            "score": 88,
            "classification": {"primary_category": "住宅"},
            "dimensions": {"composition": 4},
            "production_fields": {"title": "现代客厅", "tags": ["客厅", "现代"]},
            "image_quality": {"quality_severity": "normal"},
            "media_form": {"rendering": {"status": "yes"}},
            "provenance": {
                "asset_id": 101,
                "asset_sha256": "c" * 64,
                "strategy_bundle_id": 7,
                "model_id": "quality-model-v2",
                "rubric_version": "R3",
                "engine_version": "E2",
            },
            "raw_response": "provider secret",
            "human_review": {"reviewer": "must-not-project"},
            "candidate_mechanism": {"id": 999},
        }
        db.add(
            PublishedLabel(
                release_id=published_release.id,
                content_key=published_release.content_key,
                category_key=published_release.category_key,
                version=1,
                label_schema_version=published_release.label_schema_version,
                label_payload_json=json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False),
                payload_hash="d" * 64,
                status="published",
                published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
        )
        db.commit()

    def override_db() -> Iterator[Session]:
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_db
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: user
    try:
        yield {"client": TestClient(app), "sessions": sessions}
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        engine.dispose()


def test_projection_registry_is_versioned_and_manifest_reads_only_formal_facts() -> None:
    with _projection_context() as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        created = client.post("/api/projection-contracts", json=_contract_payload())

        assert created.status_code == 200, created.text
        contract = created.json()
        assert contract["version"] == 1
        assert contract["status"] == "draft"

        next_version = client.post("/api/projection-contracts", json=_contract_payload())
        assert next_version.status_code == 200
        assert next_version.json()["version"] == 2

        listed = client.get("/api/projection-contracts")
        assert listed.status_code == 200
        assert [item["version"] for item in listed.json()["items"]] == [2, 1]

        manifest = client.post(
            f"/api/projection-contracts/{contract['id']}/manifest"
        )
        assert manifest.status_code == 200, manifest.text
        body = manifest.json()
        assert body["row_count"] == 1
        assert body["content_keys"] == ["content:formal-1"]
        assert len(body["payload_hash"]) == 64
        assert len(body["manifest_hash"]) == 64
        assert body["input_versions"]["label_release_versions"] == [1]
        assert body["input_versions"]["asset_versions"] == ["c" * 64]
        assert body["input_versions"]["mechanism_versions"] == ["strategy-bundle:7"]
        assert body["input_versions"]["model_versions"] == ["quality-model-v2"]
        serialized = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False).lower()
        assert "candidate-1" not in serialized
        assert "provider secret" not in serialized
        assert "must-not-project" not in serialized

        repeated = client.post(
            f"/api/projection-contracts/{contract['id']}/manifest"
        )
        assert repeated.status_code == 200
        assert repeated.json()["id"] == body["id"]
        assert repeated.json()["manifest_hash"] == body["manifest_hash"]

        assert body["unexpected_count"] == 0
        assert body["payload_hash"] == manifest["payload_hash"]
        assert body["version_match"] is True
        assert body["target_table"] == "unified_dimension_table"

def test_projection_drift_detection_never_mutates_canonical_labels() -> None:
    with _projection_context() as fixture:
        client = fixture["client"]
        contract_payload = client.post(
            "/api/projection-contracts", json=_contract_payload()
        ).json()
        client.post(
            f"/api/projection-contracts/{contract_payload['id']}/reconcile"
        )

        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:
            canonical_before = [
                (item.id, item.payload_hash, item.label_payload_json, item.status)
                for item in db.scalars(
                    select(PublishedLabel).order_by(PublishedLabel.id)
                ).all()
            ]
            contract = db.get(ProjectionContract, contract_payload["id"])
            manifest = db.scalar(
                select(ProjectionManifest)
                .where(ProjectionManifest.contract_id == contract.id)
                .order_by(ProjectionManifest.id.desc())
            )
            local_row = db.scalar(
                select(LocalProjectionRow).where(
                    LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name
                )
            )
            assert contract is not None and manifest is not None and local_row is not None
            local_row.payload_json = json.dumps(
                {"content_key": "content:formal-1", "level": "tampered"}
            )
            local_row.payload_hash = "0" * 64
            db.commit()

            result = LocalProjectionAdapter().reconcile(
                db,
                contract=contract,
                manifest=manifest,
            )
            canonical_after = [
                (item.id, item.payload_hash, item.label_payload_json, item.status)
                for item in db.scalars(
                    select(PublishedLabel).order_by(PublishedLabel.id)
                ).all()
            ]

        assert result.status == "drift"
        assert result.reason == "payload_hash_mismatch"
        assert canonical_after == canonical_before


def test_semantic_projection_reads_structured_v2_facts_and_versions() -> None:
    with _projection_context() as fixture:
        with fixture["sessions"]() as db:

                    "object": {"status": "optional", "values": [{"value": "沙发", "entity_id": "object.sofa", "localized_names": {"zh": "沙发", "en": "Sofa"}, "weight": 0.7}, {"value": "茶几", "entity_id": "object.table", "localized_names": {"zh": "茶几", "en": "Coffee table"}, "weight": 0.3}]},

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_contract_merge.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.audit import canonical_json


def _contract(name: str, properties: dict, required: list[str] | None = None) -> dict:
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": name,
        "description": name,
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": properties,
            "required": required if required is not None else list(properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }


def _assert_closed(schema: dict) -> None:
    if schema.get("type") == "object":
        assert schema["additionalProperties"] is False
        assert schema["required"] == sorted(schema["properties"])
        for child in schema["properties"].values():
            _assert_closed(child)
    elif schema.get("type") == "array":
        _assert_closed(schema["items"])


def test_merge_keeps_all_fixed_fields_and_closes_every_object() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import merge_strict_prompt_contracts

    first = _contract(
        "call_a",
        {
            "classification": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"category": {"type": "string"}},
                "required": ["category"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
            "score": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 0, "maximum": 100},
        },
    )
    second = _contract(
        "call_b",
        {
            "classification": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"style": {"type": "string"}},
                "required": ["style"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
            "dimensions": {
                "type": "array",
                "items": {
                    "type": "object",
                    "properties": {
                        "name": {"type": "string"},
                        "grade": {"type": "integer"},
                    },
                    "required": ["name", "grade"],
                    "additionalProperties": False,
                },
            },
        },
    )

    merged = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [first, second],
        name="combined_result",
        description="A 与 B 的固定字段并集",
    )

    assert list(merged["schema"]["properties"]) == [
        "classification",
        "dimensions",
        "score",
    ]
    assert list(merged["schema"]["properties"]["classification"]["properties"]) == [
        "category",
        "style",
    ]
    _assert_closed(merged["schema"])


def test_merge_rejects_same_path_with_different_types() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        PromptContractMergeError,
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
    )

    with pytest.raises(PromptContractMergeError) as caught:
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
            [
                _contract("a", {"score": {"type": "integer"}}),
                _contract("b", {"score": {"type": "string"}}),
            ],
            name="combined",
        )

    assert caught.value.code == "prompt_contract_field_conflict"
    assert caught.value.path == "$.score"


def test_merge_rejects_same_path_with_different_enum_semantics() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        PromptContractMergeError,
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
    )

    with pytest.raises(PromptContractMergeError) as caught:
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
            [
                _contract(
                    "a",
                    {"level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["L1", "L2"]}},
                ),
                _contract(
                    "b",
                    {"level": {"type": "string", "enum": ["L1", "L3"]}},
                ),
            ],
            name="combined",
        )

    assert caught.value.code == "prompt_contract_field_conflict"
    assert caught.value.path == "$.level"


def test_merge_hash_is_independent_of_input_contract_order() -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
        prompt_contract_hash,
    )

    first = _contract("a", {"alpha": {"type": "string"}})
    second = _contract("b", {"beta": {"type": "integer"}})
    ab = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [first, second],
        name="combined",
        description="fixed",
    )
    ba = merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
        [deepcopy(second), deepcopy(first)],
        name="combined",
        description="fixed",
    )

    assert canonical_json(ab) == canonical_json(ba)
    assert prompt_contract_hash(ab) == prompt_contract_hash(ba)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "mutation",
    [
        {"mode": "json_object"},
        {"strict": False},
        {"schema": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}},
    ],
)
def test_merge_rejects_non_strict_or_non_object_contracts(mutation: dict) -> None:
    from app.prompt_contract_merge import (
        PromptContractMergeError,
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts,
    )

    contract = _contract("a", {"score": {"type": "integer"}})
    contract.update(mutation)

    with pytest.raises(PromptContractMergeError) as caught:
        merge_strict_prompt_contracts([contract], name="combined")

    assert caught.value.code == "prompt_contract_invalid"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_kg_rejection_evidence.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.doubao import DoubaoResponse
from app.prompt_output_contract import PromptOutputContract
from app.workflow_model_executor import (
    WorkflowModelExecutorError,
    real_prompt_node_result,
)


def _overseas_single(color: str) -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "res_id": "asset-77",
        "is_single": 2,
        "title": "Modern Oak Chair",
        "space": "",
        "object": "chair",
        "style": "modern minimal",
        "material": "wood",
        "structural_features": "with arms",
        "architectural_element": "",
        "soft_decoration": "",
        "hard_decoration": "",
        "color": color,
        "form": "geometric",
        "structure": "single",
    }


def test_prompt_step_kg_rejection_keeps_model_output_as_evidence() -> None:
    """提示词节点校验失败时，被拒的模型输出必须随异常带出去落 checkpoint。

    run159 九条失败全是「color 最多允许 1 个值」，可失败点在提示词节点，
    昨天只给投影节点挂了证据，排查时仍然看不到模型给了哪几个颜色。
    """

    client_config = SimpleNamespace(
        thinking_mode="disabled",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428",
        base_url="https://ark.example/api/v3",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_retries=1,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        name="主评测模型",
    )
    prepared = SimpleNamespace(
        output_contract=PromptOutputContract(mode="none"),
        output_contract_key="kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1",
        expected_asset_id="asset-77",
        node_instance_id="prompt_overseas_single",
        invocation_key="root",
        component_hash="c" * 64,
        model_snapshot_hash="m" * 64,
        output_budget=4096,
        reasoning_effort=None,
        client_config=client_config,
    )
    response = DoubaoResponse(
        parsed=_overseas_single("white,black"),
        raw_text="{}",
        raw_payload={},
        output_budget=4096,
        reasoning_effort=None,
        thinking_mode="disabled",
        input_tokens=1200,
        output_tokens=180,
        total_tokens=1380,
        input_image_bytes=1024,
        input_image_tokens=600,
    )

    with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as captured:
        real_prompt_node_result(prepared, response)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    assert captured.value.code == "kg_output_value_invalid"
    assert "color" in str(captured.value)
    evidence = captured.value.evidence
    assert evidence["contract_key"] == "kg.knowledge_graph.overseas.single.v1"
    assert evidence["rejected_model_output"]["color"] == "white,black"
    assert evidence["rejected_model_output"]["res_id"] == "asset-77"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_lab.py =====
"""提示词实验室（给运营 Codex 的受限接口）：只改提示词、只跑开放类目、令牌独立、并发与每日上限。"""
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

TOKEN = "t" * 40


def test_candidate_api_can_freeze_additive_output_fields(lab):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    node = next(n for n in source["prompt_nodes"] if n["node_id"] == "prompt_overseas_single")
    from copy import deepcopy
    schema = deepcopy(node["output_contract"]["output_schema"])
    schema["properties"]["review_required"] = {"type": "boolean"}
    schema["properties"]["view_angle"] = {"type": "string", "enum": ["front", ""]}
    schema["required"] += ["review_required", "view_angle"]
    body = {"source_version_id": source["id"], "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {}, "output_schemas": {node["node_id"]: schema}}
    response = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    result = response.json()
    frozen = next(n for n in result["mechanism"]["prompt_nodes"] if n["node_id"] == node["node_id"])
    assert frozen["output_contract"]["output_schema"] == schema
    assert frozen["output_contract"]["contract_hash"] != node["output_contract"]["contract_hash"]
    assert lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body).json()["candidate_version_id"] == result["candidate_version_id"]
    run = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": result["candidate_version_id"], "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert run.status_code == 201, run.text
    lab.drain()
    assert lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run.json()['id']}").json()["status"] == "completed"
    items = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run.json()['id']}/items").json()["items"]
    overseas = next(item for item in items if "overseas" in item["truth_contract_key"])
    assert overseas["prediction"]["review_required"] is False
    assert "view_angle" in overseas["prediction"]
    assert any(v["contract_hash"] == frozen["output_contract"]["contract_hash"] for v in overseas["output_contract_versions"])
    original = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{source['id']}").json()
    assert original == source


def test_candidate_api_rejects_unknown_permissions_and_identity_change(lab):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    body = {"source_version_id": source["id"], "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"], "prompts": {}, "activate": True}
    assert lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body).status_code == 422
    body.pop("activate")
    node = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    schema = node["output_contract"]["output_schema"]
    schema["properties"]["is_single"]["enum"] = [99]
    body["output_schemas"] = {node["node_id"]: schema}
    response = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert response.status_code == 422, response.text
    assert response.json()["detail"]["code"] == "kg_schema_protected_field"


@pytest.fixture()
def lab(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow
    import app.prompt_lab as prompt_lab
    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor
    from app.prompt_lab_api import build_prompt_lab_router

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'lab.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", sessions)
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "MODEL_EXECUTION_MODE", "simulated")
    with sessions() as db:
        seed_defaults(db)
        workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(db, actor="test", category_key="knowledge_graph")
        baseline = BaselineSet(category_key="knowledge_graph", name="kg lab", description="", default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="b" * 64, created_by="test")
        db.add(baseline); db.flush()
        item_ids = []
        for n, (site, scope) in enumerate([("domestic", "whole"), ("overseas", "single")]):
            contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(f"kg.knowledge_graph.{site}.{scope}.v1")
            asset = Asset(original_name=f"{site}-{scope}.jpg", stored_name=f"{n}.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=100, sha256=f"{n}" * 64, category_key="knowledge_graph")
            db.add(asset); db.flush()
            truth = {field: "" for field in contract.field_order}
            truth.update({contract.identity_field: f"asset-{n}", "is_single": contract.is_single, "color": "gray"})
            item = BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id, expected_level=None, expected_result_json=canonical_json(truth),
                truth_contract_key=contract.contract_key,
                asset_snapshot_json=canonical_json({"asset_id": asset.id, "sha256": asset.sha256, "workflow_input": {
                    "source": site, "is_single": contract.is_single, contract.identity_field: f"asset-{n}", "fixture_key": f"kg.{site}.{scope}.v1"}}))
            db.add(item); db.flush()
            item_ids.append(item.id)
        other = Asset(original_name="x.jpg", stored_name="x.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg", size_bytes=1, sha256="f" * 64, category_key="model_3d_su")
        db.add(other)
        db.commit()
        ids = SimpleNamespace(workflow=workflow.id, workflow_hash=workflow.canonical_hash, baseline=baseline.id, items=item_ids, other_asset=other.id)

    settings = SimpleNamespace(prompt_lab_token=TOKEN)
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_prompt_lab_router(lambda: settings))

    def db_override():
        db = sessions()
        try:
            yield db
        finally:
            db.close()

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    client = TestClient(app)
    client.headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {TOKEN}"

    def drain():
        while executor.process_runtime_step_once("lab-test"):
            pass

    yield SimpleNamespace(client=client, sessions=sessions, ids=ids, settings=settings, drain=drain)
    engine.dispose()


def test_token_is_separate_and_required(lab):
    assert lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 200
    anonymous = TestClient(lab.client.app)
    assert anonymous.get("/api/prompt-lab/capabilities").status_code == 401
    permissions = lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/capabilities").json()
    assert "candidates:output_schema_write" in permissions["permissions"]
    assert permissions["production_activation"] is False
    assert anonymous.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 401
    assert anonymous.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide", headers={"Authorization": "Bearer " + "x" * 40}).status_code == 401
    lab.settings.prompt_lab_token = "short"
    assert lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 503
    lab.settings.prompt_lab_token = None
    assert lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/guide").status_code == 503


def test_candidate_changes_only_prompt_text_and_is_idempotent(lab):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    nodes = {n["node_id"]: n for n in source["prompt_nodes"]}
    assert {"prompt_domestic_whole", "prompt_overseas_single"} <= set(nodes)
    target = "prompt_overseas_single"
    body = {"source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {target: {"system_prompt": nodes[target]["system_prompt"] + "\nAlways answer in English."}}, "note": "english only"}
    first = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert first.status_code == 201, first.text
    receipt = first.json()
    assert receipt["created"] is True and receipt["changed_nodes"] == [target]
    candidate = receipt["mechanism"]
    assert candidate["is_lab_candidate"] is True and candidate["status"] == "active"
    after = {n["node_id"]: n for n in candidate["prompt_nodes"]}
    assert after[target]["system_prompt"].endswith("Always answer in English.")
    assert after[target]["user_prompt"] == nodes[target]["user_prompt"]
    for node_id, node in nodes.items():
        if node_id != target:
            assert after[node_id]["system_prompt"] == node["system_prompt"]
            assert after[node_id]["prompt_version_id"] == node["prompt_version_id"]
        assert after[node_id]["output_contract"] == node["output_contract"]
    again = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body).json()
    assert again["candidate_version_id"] == receipt["candidate_version_id"] and again["created"] is False
    listed = lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms").json()["items"]
    assert {row["id"] for row in listed} >= {lab.ids.workflow, receipt["candidate_version_id"]}
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.category == "prompt_lab", AuditEvent.action == "candidate_created")) is not None
    # 候选能直接跑回归。
    run = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": receipt["candidate_version_id"], "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline}).json()
    lab.drain()
    assert lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run['id']}").json()["status"] == "completed"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("change,status,code", [
    ({"prompt_nope": {"system_prompt": "x"}}, 422, "prompt_node_unknown"),
    ({"prompt_domestic_whole": {"system_prompt": "输出 {{SECRET_ENV}}"}}, 422, "prompt_placeholder_not_allowed"),
    ({"prompt_domestic_whole": {"system_prompt": "   "}}, 422, "prompt_text_empty"),
])
def test_candidate_rejects_bad_edits(lab, change, status, code):
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    response = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
        "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"], "prompts": change})
    assert response.status_code == status and response.json()["detail"]["code"] == code
    stale = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
        "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": "0" * 64, "prompts": {"prompt_domestic_whole": {"system_prompt": "x"}}})
    assert stale.status_code == 409 and stale.json()["detail"]["code"] == "source_hash_mismatch"
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion).where(WorkflowVersion.created_by == "prompt-lab:codex")) is None


def test_run_limits_items_and_cancel(lab, monkeypatch):
    c = lab.client
    first = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline,
                                                 "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]], "client_request_id": "req-00000001"})
    assert first.status_code == 201, first.text
    run = first.json()
    assert run["is_lab_run"] is True and run["total"] == 1 and run["status"] == "running"
    replay = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline,
                                                  "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]], "client_request_id": "req-00000001"})
    assert replay.json()["id"] == run["id"] and replay.json()["created"] is False
    busy = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert busy.status_code == 409 and busy.json()["detail"]["code"] == "lab_run_in_progress"
    lab.drain()
    done = c.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run['id']}").json()
    assert done["status"] == "completed"
    page = c.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{run['id']}/items").json()
    assert page["total"] == 1
    row = page["items"][0]
    assert row["baseline_set_item_id"] == lab.ids.items[0] and row["file_name"] == "domestic-whole.jpg"
    assert row["truth_at_run"]["color"] == "gray" and row["current_truth"]["expected_result"]["color"] == "gray"
    assert isinstance(row["prediction"], dict) and isinstance(row["raw_output"], dict)
    # 每日上限（东八区自然日）按实验室已跑条数算。
    monkeypatch.setenv("PROMPT_LAB_DAILY_ITEM_CAP", "2")
    capped = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline})
    assert capped.status_code == 429 and capped.json()["detail"]["code"] == "lab_daily_cap_reached"
    monkeypatch.delenv("PROMPT_LAB_DAILY_ITEM_CAP")
    second = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline}).json()
    cancelled = c.post(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{second['id']}/cancel")
    assert cancelled.status_code == 200 and cancelled.json()["status"] == "failed"
    assert [r["id"] for r in c.get("/api/prompt-lab/runs?lab_only=true").json()["items"]] == [second["id"], run["id"]]


def test_cannot_cancel_or_read_outside_the_lab_boundary(lab):
    from app.native_baseline_regression import create_native_baseline_run
    from app.prompt_lab import PromptLabError, require_asset

    with lab.sessions() as db:
        operator_run = create_native_baseline_run(db, baseline_set=db.get(BaselineSet, lab.ids.baseline),
            workflow_version=db.get(WorkflowVersion, lab.ids.workflow), actor="liangjx", idempotency_key="page-run")
        db.commit()
        operator_run_id = operator_run.id
        with pytest.raises(PromptLabError) as denied:
            require_asset(db, lab.ids.other_asset)
        assert denied.value.status_code == 403
        kg_asset = db.get(BaselineSetItem, lab.ids.items[0]).asset_id
        assert require_asset(db, kg_asset).id == kg_asset
    refused = lab.client.post(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{operator_run_id}/cancel")
    assert refused.status_code == 403 and refused.json()["detail"]["code"] == "run_not_owned"
    # 运营在页面上跑的回归只读可见（用来读纠偏轮），不能取消。
    assert lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/runs/{operator_run_id}").json()["is_lab_run"] is False
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(BaselineRegressionRun, operator_run_id).status == "running"


def test_pricing_update_after_source_freeze_does_not_block_candidates(lab, monkeypatch):
    """.137 实测：价目表升级后重新冻结老机制只有计费快照不同，不能因此拒绝候选；模型本身变了仍要拒绝。"""
    import app.workflow_v3 as workflow_v3
    from app.models import ModelConfig, WorkflowVersion
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    original = workflow_v3.build_model_config_snapshot

    def repriced(config):
        snapshot = original(config)
        snapshot["pricing_contract"] = {"schema_version": "model-pricing-v3", "source_url": "https://example.invalid/v3"}
        return snapshot

    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_v3, "build_model_config_snapshot", repriced)
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(db.get(WorkflowVersion, lab.ids.workflow))
        node = next(n for n in manifest["nodes"] if "prompt_version_id" in (n.get("config") or {}))
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["source_model_config_id"])
    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    assert all(n["model"]["provider"] for n in source["prompt_nodes"])
    target = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    body = {"source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {target["node_id"]: {"system_prompt": target["system_prompt"] + "\n补一句", "user_prompt": ""}}}
    created = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
    assert next(n for n in created.json()["mechanism"]["prompt_nodes"] if n["node_id"] == target["node_id"])["user_prompt"] == ""
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        frozen = persisted_workflow_manifest(db.get(WorkflowVersion, created.json()["candidate_version_id"]))
        assert all(n["config"]["model_snapshot"]["runtime_config"]["pricing_contract"]["schema_version"] == "model-pricing-v3"
                   for n in frozen["nodes"] if "prompt_version_id" in (n.get("config") or {}))
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, config.id)
        config.name = "renamed-model-config"
        db.commit()
    body["prompts"][target["node_id"]]["system_prompt"] += "再补一句"
    changed = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert changed.status_code == 409 and changed.json()["detail"]["code"] == "candidate_differs_beyond_prompts"
    # 模型换成冻结不了的型号：返回 409 并说明原因，不是 500，也不留下孤儿提示词版本。
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        before_prompts = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion))
        config = db.get(ModelConfig, config.id)
        config.model_id = "some-unknown-vision-model"
        db.commit()
    body["prompts"][target["node_id"]]["system_prompt"] += "第三句"
    unfreezable = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json=body)
    assert unfreezable.status_code == 409, unfreezable.text
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(PromptVersion)) == before_prompts


def _second_set(lab):
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        second = BaselineSet(category_key="knowledge_graph", name="kg lab 2", description="", default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="c" * 64, created_by="test")
        db.add(second); db.flush()
        src = db.get(BaselineSetItem, lab.ids.items[1])
        db.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=second.id, asset_id=src.asset_id, expected_level=None, expected_result_json=src.expected_result_json,
                               truth_contract_key=src.truth_contract_key, asset_snapshot_json=src.asset_snapshot_json))
        db.commit()
        return second.id


def test_concurrent_run_requests_on_different_sets_are_serialized(lab, monkeypatch):
    """ls-review-r23 R1：两个请求各选一个基准集同时进来，不能都读到"没有在跑"而各建一轮。"""
    import threading
    import time
    import app.native_baseline_regression as nbr
    from app.prompt_lab import LabWriteLock, PromptLabError, start_run

    second_id = _second_set(lab)
    original = nbr.create_native_baseline_run
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()

    def slow(*args, **kwargs):
        entered.set()
        release.wait(10)
        return original(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(nbr, "create_native_baseline_run", slow)
    results = {}

    def attempt(name, set_id):
        with lab.sessions() as db:
            try:
                with LabWriteLock(db):
                    out = start_run(db, workflow_version_id=lab.ids.workflow, baseline_set_id=set_id)
                    db.commit()
                results[name] = out["id"]
            except PromptLabError as exc:
                db.rollback()
                results[name] = exc.code

    first = threading.Thread(target=attempt, args=("a", lab.ids.baseline)); first.start()
    assert entered.wait(10)
    second = threading.Thread(target=attempt, args=("b", second_id)); second.start()
    time.sleep(0.5)
    assert "b" not in results  # 第二个请求在锁外等第一个提交
    release.set(); first.join(20); second.join(20)
    assert isinstance(results["a"], int) and results["b"] == "lab_run_in_progress"
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(BaselineRegressionRun).where(BaselineRegressionRun.created_by == "prompt-lab:codex")) == 1


def test_client_request_id_only_replays_the_identical_request(lab):
    """ls-review-r23 R2：同一个 client_request_id 换了条目或基准集，必须 409，不能返回没跑这些样本的旧轮次。"""
    c = lab.client
    base = {"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline, "client_request_id": "req-same-0001"}
    first = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]]})
    assert first.status_code == 201
    same = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[0]]})
    assert same.json()["id"] == first.json()["id"] and same.json()["created"] is False
    other_items = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_item_ids": [lab.ids.items[1]]})
    assert other_items.status_code == 409 and other_items.json()["detail"]["code"] == "client_request_id_reused"
    lab.drain()
    other_set = c.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={**base, "baseline_set_id": _second_set(lab)})
    assert other_set.status_code == 409 and other_set.json()["detail"]["code"] == "client_request_id_reused"


def test_run_items_page_does_not_query_per_item(lab):
    """ls-review-r23 S2：逐条结果按页批量取，查询次数不随每页条数增长。"""
    from sqlalchemy import event
    from app.prompt_lab import run_items

    run = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/runs", json={"workflow_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "baseline_set_id": lab.ids.baseline}).json()
    lab.drain()
    counts = {}
    for limit in (1, 2):
        with lab.sessions() as db:
            statements = []
            listener = lambda conn, cursor, statement, *rest: statements.append(statement)
            engine = db.get_bind()
            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", listener)
            try:
                page = run_items(db, run["id"], limit=limit)
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", listener)
            assert len(page["items"]) == limit and all("expected_result" in row["current_truth"] for row in page["items"])
            counts[limit] = sum(1 for s in statements if s.lstrip().upper().startswith("SELECT"))
    assert counts[1] == counts[2], counts


class _FakeConn:
    def __init__(self, log, result=1, fail=False):
        self.log, self.result, self.fail = log, result, fail

    def execute(self, statement, params=None):
        self.log.append(str(statement).split("(")[0].replace("SELECT ", ""))
        if self.fail and "GET_LOCK" in str(statement):
            raise RuntimeError("lost connection mid-query")
        return SimpleNamespace(scalar=lambda: self.result)

    def invalidate(self):
        self.log.append("invalidate")

    def close(self):
        self.log.append("close")


def _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, conn):
    import app.prompt_lab as prompt_lab

    business = SimpleNamespace(dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="mysql"))
    lock_channel = SimpleNamespace(connect=lambda: conn)
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "_lock_engine", lambda engine: lock_channel if engine is business else None)
    db = SimpleNamespace(in_transaction=lambda: False, get_bind=lambda: business)
    return prompt_lab.LabWriteLock(db)


def test_mysql_named_lock_uses_its_own_channel_and_always_cleans_up(monkeypatch):
    """ls-review-r24 F1/F3：锁连接走独立通道；拿到锁时退出先 RELEASE_LOCK 再断开；
    拿不到返回 409；GET_LOCK 出异常时作废并断开，不把可能持锁的会话留着。"""
    from app.prompt_lab import PromptLabError

    log = []
    with _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, _FakeConn(log)):
        log.append("action+commit")
    assert log == ["GET_LOCK", "action+commit", "RELEASE_LOCK", "close"]
    log = []
    with pytest.raises(PromptLabError) as busy:
        _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, _FakeConn(log, result=0)).__enter__()
    assert busy.value.code == "lab_busy" and busy.value.status_code == 409 and log == ["GET_LOCK", "close"]
    log = []
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        _fake_mysql_lock(monkeypatch, _FakeConn(log, fail=True)).__enter__()
    assert log == ["GET_LOCK", "invalidate", "close"]


def test_lock_engine_is_outside_the_business_pool():
    from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool
    from app.prompt_lab import _lock_engine

    business = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    channel = _lock_engine(business)
    assert channel is not business and isinstance(channel.pool, NullPool) and _lock_engine(business) is channel


def test_mechanism_list_is_bounded_by_the_requested_page(lab):
    """Astra 全仓复审 F1：limit=1 不能把全部机制版本（含其他类目、大正文）都载入。"""
    from sqlalchemy import event
    from app.prompt_lab import list_mechanisms

    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    node = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    for i in range(3):
        created = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
            "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {node["node_id"]: {"system_prompt": node["system_prompt"] + f"\n版本 {i}"}}})
        assert created.status_code == 201
    with lab.sessions() as db:
        loaded = []
        listener = lambda session, instance: loaded.append(instance)
        event.listen(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        try:
            page = list_mechanisms(db, limit=1)
        finally:
            event.remove(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        assert len(page) == 1 and page[0]["is_lab_candidate"] is True
        versions = [obj for obj in loaded if isinstance(obj, WorkflowVersion)]
        assert len(versions) == 1, len(versions)
        listed = list_mechanisms(db, limit=10)
        assert len([v for v in listed if v["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"]) == 4
        assert any(v["category_key"] != "knowledge_graph" for v in listed)


def test_mechanism_list_reports_scan_cap_instead_of_going_silent(lab, monkeypatch):
    """Astra 复审 N1：定义允许多个类目时，冻结类目不开放的版本会被跳过；扫满上限还没凑够条数时，
    旧实现静默返回空列表且上限在页外检查。现在页内守住上限，返回 scan_capped 与 next_before_id 可续翻。"""
    import app.prompt_lab as prompt_lab
    from sqlalchemy import event

    # Keep this scan-cap probe scoped to one category; the public API now exposes all registered categories.
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "allowed_categories", lambda db: frozenset({"knowledge_graph"}))

    source = lab.client.get(f"/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms/{lab.ids.workflow}").json()
    node = source["prompt_nodes"][0]
    candidate_ids = []
    for i in range(3):
        created = lab.client.post("/api/prompt-lab/candidates", json={
            "source_version_id": lab.ids.workflow, "expected_source_hash": source["canonical_hash"],
            "prompts": {node["node_id"]: {"system_prompt": node["system_prompt"] + f"\n扫描上限 {i}"}}})
        assert created.status_code == 201
        candidate_ids.append(created.json()["candidate_version_id"])
    # 模拟同一定义下其它类目的版本：最新的三个候选冻结类目不在开放范围。
    foreign = set(candidate_ids)
    real_manifest = prompt_lab._manifest
    monkeypatch.setattr(prompt_lab, "_manifest", lambda version: (
        {"category_key": "space_image"} if version.id in foreign else real_manifest(version)))

    with lab.sessions() as db:
        loaded = []
        listener = lambda session, instance: loaded.append(instance)
        event.listen(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        try:
            first = prompt_lab.list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=5, scan_cap=2)
        finally:
            event.remove(db, "loaded_as_persistent", listener)
        assert first["items"] == [] and first["scan_capped"] is True and first["scanned"] == 2
        assert first["next_before_id"] == sorted(candidate_ids)[1]
        assert len([obj for obj in loaded if isinstance(obj, WorkflowVersion)]) == 2  # 页内守住上限
        rest = prompt_lab.list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=5, scan_cap=2, before_id=first["next_before_id"])
        assert [item["id"] for item in rest["items"]] == [lab.ids.workflow]
        # 刚好扫完：没有更早的版本，不误报不完整。
        assert rest["scan_capped"] is False and rest["next_before_id"] is None

    listed = lab.client.get("/api/prompt-lab/mechanisms", params={"limit": 1})
    assert listed.status_code == 200
    body = listed.json()
    assert set(body) >= {"items", "next_before_id", "scan_capped"}
    assert [item["id"] for item in body["items"]] == [lab.ids.workflow]
    assert body["next_before_id"] is None and body["scan_capped"] is False

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from app.prompt_loader import load_prompt_pairs, load_standalone_prompt


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def test_v21_prompt_is_split_into_two_complete_calls() -> None:
    pairs = load_prompt_pairs(PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "3d66-aesthetic-v2.1.md")
    assert set(pairs) == {"A", "B"}
    assert len(pairs["A"].system) > 2000
    assert len(pairs["B"].system) > 2000
    assert "{{image_metadata}}" in pairs["A"].user
    assert "{{precheck_json}}" in pairs["B"].user
    assert "{{rubric_version}}" in pairs["B"].user


def test_v13_split_prompts_match_runtime_contract() -> None:
    prompt_a = load_standalone_prompt(
        PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "space-precheck-v1.3-split.1.md"
    )
    prompt_b = load_standalone_prompt(
        PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "space-aesthetic-dimensions-v1.3-split.1.md"
    )
    assert len(prompt_a.system) > 4000
    assert "scope_status" in prompt_a.system
    assert "{{image_metadata}}" in prompt_a.user
    assert len(prompt_b.system) > 5000
    assert '"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"' in prompt_b.system
    assert "{{precheck_json}}" in prompt_b.user
    assert "{{rubric_version}}" in prompt_b.user

    assert "至少五个维度为5级" in prompt_b_calibration

    assert "{{precheck_json}}" in prompt_b.user
    assert "{{rubric_version}}" in prompt_b.user


def test_v14_lite2_calibration_caps_snapshot_and_damaged_quality_at_l2() -> None:
    calibration = (
        PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "space-aesthetic-v1.4-lite.2-calibration.md"
    ).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "casual_snapshot.status=yes" in calibration
    assert "slight|moderate|severe|unusable" in calibration
    assert "最高为 `L2`" in calibration


def test_inspiration_b_human_calibrated_prompt_contains_frozen_contract() -> None:
    prompt = (PROJECT_ROOT / "prompts" / "inspiration_image_call_b.txt").read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"
    )

    assert "视觉结构(权重0.10)" in prompt

    assert "主题清晰(权重0.06)" in prompt

    assert '"image_defects"' not in rev3

===== FILE backend/tests/test_prompt_optimizer.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app import dimension_deduction_bridge, optimizer
from app.database import Base
from app.doubao import DoubaoClient, DoubaoHTTPError, DoubaoResponse
from app.main import _optimization_payload
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    HumanReview,
    OptimizerConfig,
    PromptOptimizationRun,
    PromptVersion,
    SampleSet,
    SampleSetItem,
)


AUDIT_FIELDS = {
    "status",
    "attempt_count",
    "upstream_status_code",
    "request_correlation_id",
    "elapsed_ms",
    "error_type",
    "error_message",
    "output_budget",
    "reasoning_effort",
}


def _client_without_secret_lookup(config: OptimizerConfig) -> DoubaoClient:
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    client.config = config
    client.api_key = "offline-test-key"
    return client


def test_per_call_attempt_override_does_not_change_default_retry(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    config = OptimizerConfig(
        provider="openai",
        model_id="offline-optimizer-model",
        encrypted_api_key="offline-placeholder",
        max_tokens=12000,
        max_retries=1,
        structured_output=True,
    )
    client = _client_without_secret_lookup(config)
    calls: list[dict] = []

    async def fail_with_502(payload):
        calls.append(payload)
        raise DoubaoHTTPError(
            502,
            {
                "x-request-id": "req-502",
                "set-cookie": "must-not-be-audit-data",
            },
        )

    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "_post", fail_with_502)

    with pytest.raises(DoubaoHTTPError) as overridden:
        asyncio.run(
            client.chat_json(
                "system",
                "user",
                max_attempts=1,
                output_budget=2048,
                reasoning_effort="high",
                structured_output=True,
            )
        )
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert overridden.value.attempt_count == 1
    assert overridden.value.status_code == 502
    assert overridden.value.request_correlation_id == "req-502"
    assert calls[0]["max_completion_tokens"] == 2048
    assert calls[0]["reasoning_effort"] == "high"
    assert calls[0]["response_format"] == {"type": "json_object"}

    calls.clear()
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoHTTPError) as defaulted:
        asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user"))
    assert len(calls) == 2
    assert defaulted.value.attempt_count == 2


def test_http_429_exposes_only_a_bounded_retry_after_delay() -> None:
    assert DoubaoHTTPError(
        429, {"Retry-After": "17", "authorization": "must-not-leak"}
    ).retry_after_seconds == 17
    assert DoubaoHTTPError(429, {"retry-after": "999999"}).retry_after_seconds == 86400
    assert DoubaoHTTPError(429, {"Retry-After": "invalid"}).retry_after_seconds is None
    assert DoubaoHTTPError(503, {"Retry-After": "17"}).retry_after_seconds is None


def test_diagnostic_image_call_attempt_override_is_exactly_one(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    config = OptimizerConfig(
        provider="openai",
        model_id="offline-optimizer-model",
        encrypted_api_key="offline-placeholder",
        max_tokens=12000,
        max_retries=1,
        structured_output=False,
    )
    client = _client_without_secret_lookup(config)
    image_path = tmp_path / "diagnostic.jpg"
    image_path.write_bytes(b"offline-image-placeholder")
    calls: list[dict] = []

    async def fail_with_502(payload):
        calls.append(payload)
        raise DoubaoHTTPError(502, {"x-request-id": "diagnostic-502"})

    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "_post", fail_with_502)
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoHTTPError) as failure:
        asyncio.run(
            client.chat_json_images(
                "system",
                [("sample", image_path, "image/jpeg")],
                max_attempts=1,
                output_budget=2048,
                reasoning_effort="high",
                structured_output=True,
                max_image_count=1,
                max_single_image_bytes=1024,
                max_total_image_bytes=1024,
            )
        )

    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert failure.value.attempt_count == 1
    assert calls[0]["max_completion_tokens"] == 2048
    assert calls[0]["reasoning_effort"] == "high"
    assert calls[0]["response_format"] == {"type": "json_object"}


def test_image_limit_is_enforced_at_encoding_boundary(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    config = OptimizerConfig(
        provider="openai",
        model_id="offline-optimizer-model",
        encrypted_api_key="offline-placeholder",
    )
    client = _client_without_secret_lookup(config)
    image_path = tmp_path / "mutable.jpg"
    image_path.write_bytes(b"123456")
    calls = 0

    async def must_not_post(_payload):
        nonlocal calls
        calls += 1
        raise AssertionError("oversized image reached HTTP")

    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "_post", must_not_post)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="字节上限"):
        asyncio.run(
            client.chat_json_images(
                "system",
                [("sample", image_path, "image/jpeg")],
                max_attempts=1,
                max_image_count=1,
                max_single_image_bytes=5,
                max_total_image_bytes=5,
            )
        )
    assert calls == 0


def _bounded_item(tmp_path, index, decision, payload=b"image"):
    image_path = tmp_path / f"sample-{index}.jpg"
    image_path.write_bytes(payload)
    role = "target_error" if decision == "corrected" else "stable_control"
    return (
        SimpleNamespace(
            asset_id=index + 1,
            asset=SimpleNamespace(
                stored_name=image_path.name,
                sha256=f"{index:064x}",
            ),
        ),
        {
            "decision": decision,
            "sample_role": role,
            "dimension_schema": {
                "canonical_hash": "a" * 64,
            },
        },
    )


def test_diagnostic_records_are_bounded_and_role_stratified(tmp_path) -> None:
    selected = [
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, index, "corrected")
        for index in range(optimizer.MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_IMAGES + 3)
    ]
    selected.extend(
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, 100 + index, "approved")
        for index in range(2)
    )

    bounded, total_bytes, omitted_count = (
        optimizer._bounded_diagnostic_records(selected, tmp_path)
    )

    assert len(bounded) == optimizer.MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_IMAGES
    assert total_bytes == optimizer.MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_IMAGES * len(b"image")
    assert omitted_count == 5
    decisions = [record["decision"] for _, record, _ in bounded]
    assert "corrected" in decisions
    assert "approved" in decisions


def test_invalid_early_records_are_backfilled_from_full_role_pools(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    corrected = [
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, index, "corrected") for index in range(40)
    ]
    controls = [
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, 100 + index, "approved") for index in range(10)
    ]
    records = {
        item.asset_id: record for item, record in corrected + controls
    }
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        optimizer,
        "_review_record",
        lambda item: dict(records[item.asset_id]),
    )

    selected, _holdout_ids, eligible_count = optimizer._select_records(
        [item for item, _ in corrected + controls]
    )
    assert eligible_count == 50
    assert len(selected) == 40
    selected_corrected = [
        pair for pair in selected if pair[1]["decision"] == "corrected"
    ]
    selected_controls = [
        pair for pair in selected if pair[1]["decision"] == "approved"
    ]
    assert len(selected_corrected) == 32
    assert len(selected_controls) == 8
    for item, _ in selected_corrected[:-1] + selected_controls[:-1]:
        (tmp_path / item.asset.stored_name).unlink()

    bounded, _total_bytes, omitted_count = (
        optimizer._bounded_diagnostic_records(selected, tmp_path)
    )

    assert omitted_count == 38
    assert sorted(record["decision"] for _, record, _ in bounded) == [
        "approved",
        "corrected",
    ]


def test_optimizer_rejects_mixed_dimension_semantics(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    first = _bounded_item(tmp_path, 0, "corrected")
    second = _bounded_item(tmp_path, 1, "approved")
    records = {
        first[0].asset_id: first[1],
        second[0].asset_id: {
            **second[1],
            "dimension_schema": {"canonical_hash": "b" * 64},
        },
    }
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        optimizer,
        "_review_record",
        lambda item: dict(records[item.asset_id]),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="不能混用不同 DimensionSchema",
    ):
        optimizer._select_records([first[0], second[0]])


def test_diagnostic_records_respect_aggregate_byte_limit(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(optimizer, "MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_IMAGE_BYTES", 10)
    selected = [
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, 0, "corrected", b"123456"),
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, 1, "corrected", b"123456"),
        _bounded_item(tmp_path, 2, "corrected", b"123456"),
    ]

    bounded, total_bytes, omitted_count = (
        optimizer._bounded_diagnostic_records(selected, tmp_path)
    )

    assert len(bounded) == 1
    assert total_bytes == 6
    assert omitted_count == 2


def _create_optimizer_run(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'optimizer.db'}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session_factory = sessionmaker(
        bind=engine,
        expire_on_commit=False,
        autoflush=False,
    )

    image_path = tmp_path / "optimizer-sample.jpg"
    image_path.write_bytes(b"offline-image-placeholder")

    with session_factory() as db:
        prompt = PromptVersion(
            stage="B",
            name="base prompt",
            version="B-offline-1",
            system_prompt="base system",
            user_prompt="base user",
            status="published",
        )
        sample_set = SampleSet(name="optimizer samples")
        asset = Asset(
            original_name="sample.jpg",
            stored_name=image_path.name,
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=image_path.stat().st_size,
            sha256="a" * 64,
        )
        config = OptimizerConfig(
            encrypted_api_key="offline-placeholder",
            max_retries=1,
            structured_output=False,
        )
        db.add_all([prompt, sample_set, asset, config])
        db.flush()

        job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, status="completed")
        db.add(job)
        db.flush()
        result = EvaluationResult(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            job_id=job.id,
            precheck_json="{}",
            aesthetic_json=json.dumps(
                {
                    "dimensions": {
                        "composition_viewpoint": {
                            "score": 3,
                            "evidence": "offline evidence",
                        }
                    }
                }
            ),
            scoring_json="{}",
            raw_response_a="{}",
            raw_response_b="{}",
            score=70,
            level="L3",
            confidence=0.8,
            needs_review=False,
            model_id="offline-evaluation-model",
            prompt_a_version="A-offline-1",
            prompt_b_version=prompt.version,
            rubric_version="rubric-offline-1",
            engine_version="engine-offline-1",
        )
        db.add(result)
        db.flush()
        review = HumanReview(
            evaluation_id=result.id,
            reviewer_name="offline-reviewer",
            decision="corrected",
            corrected_level="L2",
            note="composition was overrated",
            corrections_json=json.dumps(
                [
                    {
                        "target_type": "dimension",
                        "field_key": "composition_viewpoint",
                        "model_value": 3,
                        "human_value": 2,
                    }
                ]
            ),
        )
        item = SampleSetItem(
            sample_set_id=sample_set.id,
            asset_id=asset.id,
            source_result_id=result.id,
        )
        db.add_all([review, item])
        db.flush()
        run = PromptOptimizationRun(
            base_prompt_id=prompt.id,
            sample_set_id=sample_set.id,
            optimizer_model_id=config.model_id,
            created_by="offline-test",
        )
        db.add(run)
        db.commit()
        return engine, session_factory, run.id


def test_review_record_uses_result_bound_dimension_names(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine, session_factory, _run_id = _create_optimizer_run(tmp_path)
    definition = {
        "format_version": "dimension-schema-definition-v1",
        "dimensions": [
            {
                "key": "presentation_integrity",
                "label": "呈现质量",
            },
            {
                "key": "visual_hierarchy",
                "label": "视觉组织",
            },
            {
                "key": "inspiration_reference",
                "label": "参考价值",
            },
        ],
        "output_contract": {
            "dimension_output_keys": [
                "presentation_integrity",
                "visual_hierarchy",
                "inspiration_reference",
            ],
            "unknown_key_policy": "reject",
        },
    }
    try:
        with session_factory() as db:
            item = db.scalar(select(SampleSetItem))
            result = item.source_result
            result.strategy_snapshot_json = json.dumps(
                {
                    "schema_version": "strategy-bundle-v2",
                    "resolved_dimension_schema_id": 71,
                    "resolved_dimension_schema_key": "dimension.test",
                    "resolved_dimension_schema_version": "test-v1",
                    "resolved_dimension_schema_hash": (
                        optimizer.canonical_hash(definition)
                    ),
                    "resolved_dimensions_snapshot": definition,
                },
                ensure_ascii=False,
            )
            result.aesthetic_json = json.dumps(
                {
                    "dimensions": {
                        dimension["key"]: {"grade": 3}
                        for dimension in definition["dimensions"]
                    }
                },
                ensure_ascii=False,
            )
            record = optimizer._review_record(item)

        assert record is not None
        assert record["dimension_names"] == {
            "presentation_integrity": "呈现质量",
            "visual_hierarchy": "视觉组织",
            "inspiration_reference": "参考价值",
        }
        assert record["dimension_schema"] == {
            "schema_id": 71,
            "schema_key": "dimension.test",
            "version": "test-v1",
            "canonical_hash": optimizer.canonical_hash(definition),
        }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _install_optimizer_dependencies(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    session_factory,
    fake_client_type,
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(optimizer, "SessionLocal", session_factory)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        optimizer,
        "get_settings",
        lambda: SimpleNamespace(upload_dir=tmp_path),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(optimizer, "DoubaoClient", fake_client_type)


def test_oversized_diagnostic_image_fails_before_client_call(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, session_factory, run_id = _create_optimizer_run(tmp_path)
    image_path = tmp_path / "optimizer-sample.jpg"
    image_path.write_bytes(
        b"x" * (optimizer.MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
    )

    class ClientMustNotRun:
        calls = 0

        def __init__(self, _config):
            pass

        async def chat_json_images(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            type(self).calls += 1
            raise AssertionError("oversized input reached the HTTP client")

    _install_optimizer_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        session_factory,
        ClientMustNotRun,
    )
    try:
        asyncio.run(optimizer.run_prompt_optimization(run_id))
        with session_factory() as db:
            run = db.get(PromptOptimizationRun, run_id)
            audit = json.loads(run.diagnostic_audit_json)
            assert run.status == "failed"
            assert run.candidate_system_prompt == ""
            assert run.candidate_user_prompt == ""
            assert audit["status"] == "failed"
            assert audit["attempt_count"] == 0
            assert audit["error_type"] == "invalid_stage_output"
        assert ClientMustNotRun.calls == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_encoding_boundary_rejection_persists_zero_attempts(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, session_factory, run_id = _create_optimizer_run(tmp_path)
    original_bounded = optimizer._bounded_diagnostic_records

    def mutate_after_metadata_check(selected, upload_dir):
        bounded = original_bounded(selected, upload_dir)
        image_path = bounded[0][0][2]
        image_path.write_bytes(
            b"x" * (optimizer.MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_SINGLE_IMAGE_BYTES + 1)
        )
        return bounded

    class EncodingBoundaryClient(DoubaoClient):
        post_calls = 0

        def __init__(self, config):
            self.config = config
            self.api_key = "offline-test-key"

        async def _post(self, _payload):
            type(self).post_calls += 1
            raise AssertionError("encoding-boundary rejection reached HTTP")

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        optimizer,
        "_bounded_diagnostic_records",
        mutate_after_metadata_check,
    )
    _install_optimizer_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        tmp_path,
        session_factory,
        EncodingBoundaryClient,
    )
    try:
        asyncio.run(optimizer.run_prompt_optimization(run_id))
        with session_factory() as db:
            run = db.get(PromptOptimizationRun, run_id)
            audit = json.loads(run.diagnostic_audit_json)
            assert run.status == "failed"
            assert audit["status"] == "failed"
            assert audit["attempt_count"] == 0
            assert audit["error_type"] == "invalid_stage_output"
            assert run.candidate_system_prompt == ""
            assert run.candidate_user_prompt == ""
        assert EncodingBoundaryClient.post_calls == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_diagnosis_and_audit_persist_when_synthesis_fails(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    engine, session_factory, run_id = _create_optimizer_run(tmp_path)

    class FailingSynthesisClient:
        diagnostic_calls = 0
        synthesis_calls = 0

        def __init__(self, config):
            assert config.max_retries == 1

        async def chat_json_images(self, _system, samples, **options):
            type(self).diagnostic_calls += 1
            assert len(samples) == 1
            assert options == {
                "max_attempts": 1,
                "output_budget": 2048,
                "reasoning_effort": "high",
                "structured_output": True,
                "max_image_count": 8,
                "max_single_image_bytes": 16 * 1024 * 1024,
                "max_total_image_bytes": 32 * 1024 * 1024,
            }
            return DoubaoResponse(
                parsed={
                    "summary": "persisted diagnosis",
                    "cases": [],
                    "patterns": ["overrating"],
                    "prompt_risks": [],
                },
                raw_text='{"summary":"persisted diagnosis"}',
                raw_payload={
                    "body": "bulk upstream response must not enter audit",
                    "headers": {"set-cookie": "secret"},
                },
                upstream_status_code=200,
                request_correlation_id="diag-request-1",
                attempt_count=1,
                output_budget=2048,
                reasoning_effort="high",
            )

        async def chat_json(self, _system, _user, **options):
            type(self).synthesis_calls += 1
            assert options == {
                "max_attempts": 1,
                "output_budget": 4096,
                "reasoning_effort": "high",
                "structured_output": True,
            }
            with session_factory() as verification:
                persisted = verification.get(PromptOptimizationRun, run_id)
                diagnosis = json.loads(persisted.diagnosis_json)
                diagnostic_audit = json.loads(
                    persisted.diagnostic_audit_json
                )
                assert diagnosis["summary"] == "persisted diagnosis"
                assert diagnostic_audit["status"] == "succeeded"
                assert diagnostic_audit["attempt_count"] == 1
            error = DoubaoHTTPError(
                502,
                {
                    "x-request-id": "synthesis-request-502",
                    "x-debug-body": "must-not-be-stored",
